1→import { getSupabaseClient as supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
     2→
     3→export interface TaskLogCostEntry {
     4→  task_id: string | null;
     5→  amount: number;
     6→  created_at: string;
     7→}
     8→
     9→export async function fetchTaskLogCosts(taskIds: string[]): Promise<TaskLogCostEntry[]> {
    10→  if (taskIds.length === 0) {
    11→    return [];
    12→  }
    13→
    14→  const { data: costs } = await supabase()
    15→    .from('credits_ledger')
    16→    .select('task_id, amount, created_at')
    17→    .in('task_id', taskIds)
    18→    .eq('type', 'spend');
    19→
    20→  return costs || [];
    21→}
    22→
```

1→interface VideoFrameCaptureElements {
     2→  video: HTMLVideoElement;
     3→  canvas: HTMLCanvasElement;
     4→  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D;
     5→}
     6→
     7→export function createVideoFrameCaptureElements(): VideoFrameCaptureElements | null {
     8→  const video = document.createElement('video');
     9→  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    10→  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    11→
    12→  if (!ctx) {
    13→    return null;
    14→  }
    15→
    16→  return { video, canvas, ctx };
    17→}
    18→
    19→export function configureVideoForFrameCapture(video: HTMLVideoElement, crossOrigin?: string): void {
    20→  if (crossOrigin) {
    21→    video.crossOrigin = crossOrigin;
    22→  }
    23→
    24→  video.preload = 'metadata';
    25→  video.muted = true;
    26→  video.playsInline = true;
    27→}
    28→

1→import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
     2→import type { ClipPairInfo } from '@/shared/components/JoinClipsSettingsForm/types';
     3→
     4→interface UseClipPairSelectionResult {
     5→  selectedPairIndex: number;
     6→  setSelectedPairIndex: (index: number) => void;
     7→  selectedPair: ClipPairInfo | undefined;
     8→  hasPairs: boolean;
     9→  hasMultiplePairs: boolean;
    10→}
    11→
    12→export function useClipPairSelection(
    13→  clipPairs?: ClipPairInfo[]
    14→): UseClipPairSelectionResult {
    15→  const [selectedPairIndex, setSelectedPairIndex] = useState(0);
    16→  const pairCount = clipPairs?.length ?? 0;
    17→
    18→  useEffect(() => {
    19→    if (pairCount === 0) {
    20→      if (selectedPairIndex !== 0) {
    21→        setSelectedPairIndex(0);
    22→      }
    23→      return;
    24→    }
    25→    if (selectedPairIndex >= pairCount) {
    26→      setSelectedPairIndex(pairCount - 1);
    27→    }
    28→  }, [pairCount, selectedPairIndex]);
    29→
    30→  return {
    31→    selectedPairIndex,
    32→    setSelectedPairIndex,
    33→    selectedPair: clipPairs?.[selectedPairIndex],
    34→    hasPairs: pairCount > 0,
    35→    hasMultiplePairs: pairCount > 1,
    36→  };
    37→}
    38→

1→import { useMemo } from 'react';
     2→import { GRID_COLUMN_CLASSES, calculateGalleryLayout } from '../utils';
     3→
     4→interface UseAspectRatioLayoutParams {
     5→  projectAspectRatio?: string;
     6→  isMobile: boolean;
     7→  containerWidth: number;
     8→  reducedSpacing: boolean;
     9→  columnsPerRow: 'auto' | number;
    10→  itemsPerPage?: number;
    11→}
    12→
    13→export function useAspectRatioLayout({
    14→  projectAspectRatio,
    15→  isMobile,
    16→  containerWidth,
    17→  reducedSpacing,
    18→  columnsPerRow,
    19→  itemsPerPage,
    20→}: UseAspectRatioLayoutParams) {
    21→  const aspectRatioLayout = useMemo(
    22→    () => calculateGalleryLayout(projectAspectRatio, isMobile, containerWidth, undefined, reducedSpacing),
    23→    [projectAspectRatio, isMobile, containerWidth, reducedSpacing],
    24→  );
    25→
    26→  const effectiveColumnsPerRow = columnsPerRow === 'auto' ? aspectRatioLayout.columns : columnsPerRow;
    27→  const defaultItemsPerPage = aspectRatioLayout.itemsPerPage;
    28→
    29→  const rawItemsPerPage = itemsPerPage ?? defaultItemsPerPage;
    30→  const actualItemsPerPage =
    31→    Math.floor(rawItemsPerPage / effectiveColumnsPerRow) * effectiveColumnsPerRow || effectiveColumnsPerRow;
    32→
    33→  const gridColumnClasses = useMemo(
    34→    () =>
    35→      GRID_COLUMN_CLASSES[effectiveColumnsPerRow as keyof typeof GRID_COLUMN_CLASSES] ||
    36→      aspectRatioLayout.gridColumnClasses,
    37→    [effectiveColumnsPerRow, aspectRatioLayout.gridColumnClasses],
    38→  );
    39→
    40→  return {
    41→    aspectRatioLayout,
    42→    effectiveColumnsPerRow,
    43→    actualItemsPerPage,
    44→    gridColumnClasses,
    45→  };
    46→}
    47→

1→import { getSupabaseClient } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
     2→
     3→type SettingsScopeIdentifier = 'user' | 'project' | 'shot';
     4→type SettingsScopeTableName = 'users' | 'projects' | 'shots';
     5→
     6→const SETTINGS_SCOPE_TABLES: Record<SettingsScopeIdentifier, SettingsScopeTableName> = {
     7→  user: 'users',
     8→  project: 'projects',
     9→  shot: 'shots',
    10→};
    11→
    12→function assertNeverScope(scope: never): never {
    13→  throw new Error(`Unsupported settings scope: ${String(scope)}`);
    14→}
    15→
    16→export function resolveSettingsScopeTable(scope: SettingsScopeIdentifier): SettingsScopeTableName {
    17→  switch (scope) {
    18→    case 'user':
    19→    case 'project':
    20→    case 'shot':
    21→      return SETTINGS_SCOPE_TABLES[scope];
    22→    default:
    23→      return assertNeverScope(scope);
    24→  }
    25→}
    26→
    27→export function selectSettingsForScope(scope: SettingsScopeIdentifier, id: string) {
    28→  const tableName = resolveSettingsScopeTable(scope);
    29→  return getSupabaseClient()
    30→    .from(tableName)
    31→    .select('settings')
    32→    .eq('id', id)
    33→    .single();
    34→}
    35→
    36→export function callUpdateToolSettingsAtomicRpc(
    37→  tableName: SettingsScopeTableName,
    38→  id: string,
    39→  toolId: string,
    40→  settings: Record<string, unknown>,
    41→) {
    42→  return getSupabaseClient().rpc('update_tool_settings_atomic', {
    43→    p_table_name: tableName,
    44→    p_id: id,
    45→    p_tool_id: toolId,
    46→    p_settings: settings,
    47→  });
    48→}
    49→

1→import type { GeneratedImageWithMetadata } from '@/shared/components/MediaGallery/types';
     2→import { parseRatio } from '@/shared/lib/media/aspectRatios';
     3→
     4→const MIN_PADDING = 60;
     5→const MAX_PADDING = 200;
     6→
     7→function clampPadding(value: number): number {
     8→  return Math.min(Math.max(value, MIN_PADDING), MAX_PADDING);
     9→}
    10→
    11→function extractResolutionFromMetadata(image: GeneratedImageWithMetadata): { width?: number; height?: number } {
    12→  let width = image.metadata?.width;
    13→  let height = image.metadata?.height;
    14→
    15→  if (width && height) {
    16→    return { width, height };
    17→  }
    18→
    19→  const resolution = image.metadata?.originalParams?.orchestrator_details?.resolution;
    20→  if (resolution && typeof resolution === 'string' && resolution.includes('x')) {
    21→    const [parsedWidth, parsedHeight] = resolution.split('x').map(Number);
    22→    if (!isNaN(parsedWidth) && !isNaN(parsedHeight)) {
    23→      width = parsedWidth;
    24→      height = parsedHeight;
    25→    }
    26→  }
    27→
    28→  return { width, height };
    29→}
    30→
    31→export function resolveAspectRatioPadding(
    32→  image: GeneratedImageWithMetadata,
    33→  projectAspectRatio?: string,
    34→): string {
    35→  if (projectAspectRatio) {
    36→    const ratio = parseRatio(projectAspectRatio);
    37→    if (!isNaN(ratio)) {
    38→      const calculatedPadding = (1 / ratio) * 100;
    39→      return `${clampPadding(calculatedPadding)}%`;
    40→    }
    41→  }
    42→
    43→  const { width, height } = extractResolutionFromMetadata(image);
    44→  if (width && height) {
    45→    return `${clampPadding((height / width) * 100)}%`;
    46→  }
    47→
    48→  return '100%';
    49→}
    50→

1→import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
     2→import { ONBOARDING_STEPS } from '@/shared/components/OnboardingModal/data/onboardingSteps';
     3→
     4→export function useOnboardingSteps(isOpen: boolean) {
     5→  const [currentStep, setCurrentStep] = useState(1);
     6→  const [isShaking, setIsShaking] = useState(false);
     7→  const shakeTimeoutRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
     8→
     9→  useEffect(() => {
    10→    if (isOpen) {
    11→      setCurrentStep(1);
    12→    }
    13→  }, [isOpen]);
    14→
    15→  useEffect(() => {
    16→    return () => {
    17→      if (shakeTimeoutRef.current) {
    18→        clearTimeout(shakeTimeoutRef.current);
    19→      }
    20→    };
    21→  }, []);
    22→
    23→  const handleNext = () => {
    24→    setCurrentStep((previous) => Math.min(previous + 1, ONBOARDING_STEPS.length));
    25→  };
    26→
    27→  const handleBack = () => {
    28→    setCurrentStep((previous) => Math.max(previous - 1, 1));
    29→  };
    30→
    31→  const handleShake = () => {
    32→    setIsShaking(true);
    33→    if (shakeTimeoutRef.current) {
    34→      clearTimeout(shakeTimeoutRef.current);
    35→    }
    36→    shakeTimeoutRef.current = setTimeout(() => setIsShaking(false), 500);
    37→  };
    38→
    39→  const currentStepDefinition =
    40→    ONBOARDING_STEPS[currentStep - 1] ?? ONBOARDING_STEPS[0];
    41→  const stepTitles = ONBOARDING_STEPS.map((step) => step.title);
    42→
    43→  return {
    44→    currentStep,
    45→    isShaking,
    46→    handleNext,
    47→    handleBack,
    48→    handleShake,
    49→    currentStepDefinition,
    50→    stepTitles,
    51→  };
    52→}
    53→

1→/**
     2→ * Handles task generation for inpainting and annotated edits.
     3→ * Exports mask from Konva, uploads to storage, and creates tasks.
     4→ */
     5→
     6→import { useCallback } from 'react';
     7→import { toast } from '@/shared/components/ui/runtime/sonner';
     8→import type { GenerationRow } from '@/domains/generation/types';
     9→import { uploadImageToStorage } from '@/shared/lib/media/imageUploader';
    10→import { createImageInpaintTask } from '@/shared/lib/tasks/imageEditing/imageInpaint';
    11→import { createAnnotatedImageEditTask } from '@/shared/lib/tasks/imageEditing/annotatedImageEdit';
    12→import { buildMaskedEditTaskParams } from '@/shared/lib/tasks/imageEditing/buildMaskedEditTaskParams';
    13→import type { StrokeOverlayHandle } from '../../components/StrokeOverlay';
    14→import type { BrushStroke, EditAdvancedSettings, QwenEditModel } from './types';
    15→
    16→import { convertToHiresFixApiParams } from '../useGenerationEditSettings';
    17→import { getGenerationId, getMediaUrl } from '@/shared/lib/media/mediaTypeHelpers';
    18→import { useTaskPlaceholder } from '@/shared/hooks/tasks/useTaskPlaceholder';
    19→
    20→/**
    21→ * Task type configuration - captures the differences between inpaint and annotate modes
    22→ */
    23→type TaskType = 'inpaint' | 'annotate';
    24→
    25→interface TaskTypeConfig {
    26→  logPrefix: string;
    27→  emptyStrokesError: string;
    28→  overlayNotReadyError: string;
    29→  fileNamePrefix: string;
    30→  taskCreationError: string;
    31→  createTask: typeof createImageInpaintTask | typeof createAnnotatedImageEditTask;
    32→}
    33→
    34→const TASK_CONFIGS: Record<TaskType, TaskTypeConfig> = {
    35→  inpaint: {
    36→    logPrefix: '[Inpaint]',
    37→    emptyStrokesError: 'Please paint on the image first',
    38→    overlayNotReadyError: 'Paint overlay not ready',
    39→    fileNamePrefix: 'inpaint_mask',
    40→    taskCreationError: 'Failed to create inpaint task',
    41→    createTask: createImageInpaintTask,
    42→  },
    43→  annotate: {
    44→    logPrefix: '[AnnotateEdit]',
    45→    emptyStrokesError: 'Please draw an annotation rectangle',
    46→    overlayNotReadyError: 'Annotation overlay not ready',
    47→    fileNamePrefix: 'annotated_edit_mask',
    48→    taskCreationError: 'Failed to create annotated edit task',
    49→    createTask: createAnnotatedImageEditTask,
    50→  },
    51→};
    52→
    53→interface UseTaskGenerationProps {
    54→  media: GenerationRow;
    55→  selectedProjectId: string | null;
    56→  shotId?: string;
    57→  toolTypeOverride?: string;
    58→  isVideo: boolean;
    59→  loras?: Array<{ url: string; strength: number }>;
    60→  activeVariantId?: string | null;
    61→  activeVariantLocation?: string | null;
    62→  createAsGeneration?: boolean;
    63→  advancedSettings?: EditAdvancedSettings;
    64→  qwenEditModel?: QwenEditModel;
    65→  // State
    66→  inpaintStrokes: BrushStroke[];
    67→  annotationStrokes: BrushStroke[];
    68→  inpaintPrompt: string;
    69→  inpaintNumGenerations: number;
    70→  // Refs
    71→  strokeOverlayRef: React.RefObject<StrokeOverlayHandle>;
    72→  // Callbacks
    73→  handleExitInpaintMode: () => void;
    74→  setIsGeneratingInpaint: (isGenerating: boolean) => void;
    75→  setInpaintGenerateSuccess: (success: boolean) => void;
    76→}
    77→
    78→export function useTaskGeneration({
    79→  media,
    80→  selectedProjectId,
    81→  shotId,
    82→  toolTypeOverride,
    83→  isVideo,
    84→  loras,
    85→  activeVariantId,
    86→  activeVariantLocation,
    87→  createAsGeneration,
    88→  advancedSettings,
    89→  qwenEditModel,
    90→  inpaintStrokes,
    91→  annotationStrokes,
    92→  inpaintPrompt,
    93→  inpaintNumGenerations,
    94→  strokeOverlayRef,
    95→  handleExitInpaintMode,
    96→  setIsGeneratingInpaint,
    97→  setInpaintGenerateSuccess,
    98→}: UseTaskGenerationProps) {
    99→  // Get actual generation ID (may differ from media.id for shot_generations)
   100→  const actualGenerationId = getGenerationId(media);
   101→  const run = useTaskPlaceholder();
   102→
   103→  /**
   104→   * Unified task generation function
   105→   * Handles both inpaint and annotate modes with mode-specific configuration
   106→   */
   107→  const handleGenerateTask = useCallback(async (taskType: TaskType) => {
   108→    const config = TASK_CONFIGS[taskType];
   109→    const strokes = taskType === 'inpaint' ? inpaintStrokes : annotationStrokes;
   110→
   111→    // Validation
   112→    if (!selectedProjectId || isVideo) {
   113→      toast.error(`Cannot generate ${taskType === 'inpaint' ? 'inpaint' : 'annotated edit'}`);
   114→      return;
   115→    }
   116→
   117→    if (strokes.length === 0) {
   118→      toast.error(config.emptyStrokesError);
   119→      return;
   120→    }
   121→
   122→    if (!inpaintPrompt.trim()) {
   123→      toast.error('Please enter a prompt');
   124→      return;
   125→    }
   126→
   127→    if (!strokeOverlayRef.current) {
   128→      toast.error(config.overlayNotReadyError);
   129→      return;
   130→    }
   131→
   132→    const incomingTaskType = taskType === 'inpaint' ? 'image_inpaint' : 'annotated_image_edit';
   133→
   134→    setIsGeneratingInpaint(true);
   135→    try {
   136→      await run({
   137→        taskType: incomingTaskType,
   138→        label: inpaintPrompt.trim() || 'Editing...',
   139→        context: 'useTaskGeneration',
   140→        toastTitle: config.taskCreationError,
   141→        create: async () => {
   142→          // Export mask directly from Konva
   143→          const maskImageData = strokeOverlayRef.current!.exportMask({ pixelRatio: 1.5 });
   144→
   145→          if (!maskImageData) {
   146→            throw new Error('Failed to export mask from overlay');
   147→          }
   148→
   149→          // Upload mask to storage
   150→          const maskFile = await fetch(maskImageData)
   151→            .then(res => res.blob())
   152→            .then(blob => new File([blob], `${config.fileNamePrefix}_${media.id}_${Date.now()}.png`, { type: 'image/png' }));
   153→
   154→          const maskUrl = await uploadImageToStorage(maskFile);
   155→
   156→          // Get source image URL
   157→          const mediaUrl = getMediaUrl(media) || media.imageUrl;
   158→          const sourceUrl = activeVariantLocation || mediaUrl;
   159→          if (!sourceUrl) {
   160→            throw new Error('Missing source media URL');
   161→          }
   162→          if (!actualGenerationId) {
   163→            throw new Error('Missing generation id');
   164→          }
   165→
   166→          return config.createTask(
   167→            buildMaskedEditTaskParams({
   168→              projectId: selectedProjectId,
   169→              imageUrl: sourceUrl,
   170→              maskUrl,
   171→              prompt: inpaintPrompt,
   172→              numGenerations: inpaintNumGenerations,
   173→              generationId: actualGenerationId ?? undefined,
   174→              shotId,
   175→              toolType: toolTypeOverride,
   176→              loras,
   177→              createAsGeneration,
   178→              sourceVariantId: activeVariantId || undefined,
   179→              hiresFix: convertToHiresFixApiParams(advancedSettings),
   180→              qwenEditModel,
   181→            }),
   182→          );
   183→        },
   184→        onSuccess: () => {
   185→          // Show success state
   186→          setInpaintGenerateSuccess(true);
   187→
   188→          // Wait 1 second to show success, then exit
   189→          setTimeout(() => {
   190→            setInpaintGenerateSuccess(false);
   191→            handleExitInpaintMode();
   192→          }, 1000);
   193→        },
   194→      });
   195→    } finally {
   196→      setIsGeneratingInpaint(false);
   197→    }
   198→  }, [
   199→    selectedProjectId, isVideo, inpaintStrokes, annotationStrokes, inpaintPrompt, inpaintNumGenerations,
   200→    media, handleExitInpaintMode, shotId, toolTypeOverride, loras,
   201→    activeVariantLocation, activeVariantId, createAsGeneration, advancedSettings,
   202→    qwenEditModel, strokeOverlayRef, actualGenerationId,
   203→    setIsGeneratingInpaint, setInpaintGenerateSuccess, run
   204→  ]);
   205→
   206→  // Stable callbacks that preserve the original API
   207→  const handleGenerateInpaint = useCallback(() => {
   208→    return handleGenerateTask('inpaint');
   209→  }, [handleGenerateTask]);
   210→
   211→  const handleGenerateAnnotatedEdit = useCallback(() => {
   212→    return handleGenerateTask('annotate');
   213→  }, [handleGenerateTask]);
   214→
   215→  return {
   216→    handleGenerateInpaint,
   217→    handleGenerateAnnotatedEdit,

1→import { getSupabaseClient as supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
     2→import { getHiddenTaskTypes, getVisibleTaskTypes } from '@/shared/lib/tasks/taskConfig';
     3→import { fetchTaskLogCosts, type TaskLogCostEntry } from './taskLogCosts';
     4→
     5→export interface TaskLogFilters {
     6→  costFilter?: 'all' | 'free' | 'paid';
     7→  status?: string[];
     8→  taskTypes?: string[];
     9→  projectIds?: string[];
    10→}
    11→
    12→export interface TaskLogProject {
    13→  id: string;
    14→  name: string;
    15→}
    16→
    17→export interface TaskLogAvailableFilters {
    18→  taskTypes: string[];
    19→  projects: TaskLogProject[];
    20→  statuses: string[];
    21→}
    22→
    23→export interface TaskLogTaskRecord {
    24→  id: string;
    25→  task_type: string;
    26→  status: string;
    27→  created_at: string;
    28→  generation_started_at?: string | null;
    29→  generation_processed_at?: string | null;
    30→  project_id: string;
    31→}
    32→
    33→export interface EnrichedTaskLogTask {
    34→  id: string;
    35→  taskType: string;
    36→  status: string;
    37→  createdAt: string;
    38→  generationStartedAt?: string | null;
    39→  generationProcessedAt?: string | null;
    40→  projectId: string;
    41→  cost?: number;
    42→  duration?: number;
    43→  projectName?: string;
    44→}
    45→
    46→export interface TaskLogDataResult {
    47→  availableFilters: TaskLogAvailableFilters;
    48→  projects: TaskLogProject[];
    49→  tasks: EnrichedTaskLogTask[];
    50→  total: number;
    51→}
    52→
    53→interface FetchTaskLogDataOptions {
    54→  filters?: TaskLogFilters;
    55→  limit?: number;
    56→  offset?: number;
    57→}
    58→
    59→function getTaskDuration(task: TaskLogTaskRecord): number | undefined {
    60→  if (!task.generation_started_at || !task.generation_processed_at) {
    61→    return undefined;
    62→  }
    63→
    64→  const start = new Date(task.generation_started_at);
    65→  const end = new Date(task.generation_processed_at);
    66→  return Math.ceil((end.getTime() - start.getTime()) / 1000);
    67→}
    68→
    69→export function enrichTaskLogTasks(
    70→  tasks: TaskLogTaskRecord[],
    71→  costs: TaskLogCostEntry[],
    72→  projectLookup: Record<string, string>,
    73→): EnrichedTaskLogTask[] {
    74→  return tasks.map((task) => {
    75→    const costEntry = costs.find((entry) => entry.task_id === task.id);
    76→
    77→    return {
    78→      id: task.id,
    79→      taskType: task.task_type,
    80→      status: task.status,
    81→      createdAt: task.created_at,
    82→      generationStartedAt: task.generation_started_at,
    83→      generationProcessedAt: task.generation_processed_at,
    84→      projectId: task.project_id,
    85→      projectName: projectLookup[task.project_id] || 'Unknown Project',
    86→      cost: costEntry ? Math.abs(costEntry.amount) : undefined,
    87→      duration: getTaskDuration(task),
    88→    };
    89→  });
    90→}
    91→
    92→export function applyTaskLogCostFilter(
    93→  tasks: EnrichedTaskLogTask[],
    94→  costFilter: TaskLogFilters['costFilter'],
    95→): EnrichedTaskLogTask[] {
    96→  if (costFilter === 'free') {
    97→    return tasks.filter((task) => !task.cost || task.cost === 0);
    98→  }
    99→
   100→  if (costFilter === 'paid') {
   101→    return tasks.filter((task) => Boolean(task.cost && task.cost > 0));
   102→  }
   103→
   104→  return tasks;
   105→}
   106→
   107→async function requireTaskLogProjects(userId: string): Promise<TaskLogProject[]> {
   108→  const { data: projects } = await supabase().from('projects')
   109→    .select('id, name')
   110→    .eq('user_id', userId);
   111→
   112→  return projects || [];
   113→}
   114→
   115→function applyTaskLogQueryFilters(
   116→  query: {
   117→    in: (column: string, values: string[]) => unknown;
   118→    not: (column: string, operator: string, value: string) => unknown;
   119→  } & Record<string, unknown>,
   120→  filters: TaskLogFilters,
   121→  hiddenTaskTypes: string[],
   122→) {
   123→  let nextQuery = query;
   124→
   125→  if (hiddenTaskTypes.length > 0) {
   126→    nextQuery = nextQuery.not('task_type', 'in', `(${hiddenTaskTypes.join(',')})`) as typeof query;
   127→  }
   128→
   129→  if (filters.status && filters.status.length > 0) {
   130→    nextQuery = nextQuery.in('status', filters.status) as typeof query;
   131→  }
   132→
   133→  if (filters.taskTypes && filters.taskTypes.length > 0) {
   134→    nextQuery = nextQuery.in('task_type', filters.taskTypes) as typeof query;
   135→  }
   136→
   137→  if (filters.projectIds && filters.projectIds.length > 0) {
   138→    nextQuery = nextQuery.in('project_id', filters.projectIds) as typeof query;
   139→  }
   140→
   141→  return nextQuery;
   142→}
   143→
   144→async function fetchAvailableTaskLogFilters(
   145→  projectIds: string[],
   146→  projects: TaskLogProject[],
   147→  hiddenTaskTypes: string[],
   148→): Promise<TaskLogAvailableFilters> {
   149→  let availableQuery = supabase().from('tasks')
   150→    .select('task_type, status, project_id')
   151→    .in('project_id', projectIds);
   152→
   153→  if (hiddenTaskTypes.length > 0) {
   154→    availableQuery = availableQuery.not('task_type', 'in', `(${hiddenTaskTypes.join(',')})`);
   155→  }
   156→
   157→  const { data: allTasks } = await availableQuery;
   158→
   159→  return {
   160→    taskTypes: getVisibleTaskTypes().sort((left, right) => left.localeCompare(right)),
   161→    projects,
   162→    statuses: [...new Set((allTasks || []).map((task) => task.status))].sort(
   163→      (left, right) => left.localeCompare(right),
   164→    ),
   165→  };
   166→}
   167→
   168→export async function fetchTaskLogData({
   169→  filters = {},
   170→  limit,
   171→  offset,
   172→}: FetchTaskLogDataOptions = {}): Promise<TaskLogDataResult> {
   173→  const client = supabase();
   174→  const { data: { user }, error: authError } = await client.auth.getUser();
   175→
   176→  if (authError || !user) {
   177→    throw new Error('Authentication required');
   178→  }
   179→
   180→  const projects = await requireTaskLogProjects(user.id);
   181→  if (projects.length === 0) {
   182→    return {
   183→      availableFilters: {
   184→        taskTypes: [],
   185→        projects: [],
   186→        statuses: [],
   187→      },
   188→      projects: [],
   189→      tasks: [],
   190→      total: 0,
   191→    };
   192→  }
   193→
   194→  const projectIds = projects.map((project) => project.id);
   195→  const projectLookup = Object.fromEntries(projects.map((project) => [project.id, project.name]));
   196→  const hiddenTaskTypes = getHiddenTaskTypes();
   197→
   198→  let query = client.from('tasks')
   199→    .select('*', { count: 'exact' })
   200→    .in('project_id', projectIds);
   201→
   202→  query = applyTaskLogQueryFilters(query, filters, hiddenTaskTypes);
   203→
   204→  let orderedQuery = query.order('created_at', { ascending: false });
   205→  if (typeof limit === 'number' && typeof offset === 'number') {
   206→    orderedQuery = orderedQuery.range(offset, offset + limit - 1);
   207→  }
   208→
   209→  const { data: taskRows, error: tasksError, count } = await orderedQuery;
   210→
   211→  if (tasksError) {
   212→    throw new Error(`Failed to fetch tasks: ${tasksError.message}`);
   213→  }
   214→
   215→  const tasksData = (taskRows || []) as TaskLogTaskRecord[];
   216→  const taskIds = tasksData.map((task) => task.id);
   217→  const costsData = await fetchTaskLogCosts(taskIds);
   218→  const enrichedTasks = applyTaskLogCostFilter(
   219→    enrichTaskLogTasks(tasksData, costsData, projectLookup),
   220→    filters.costFilter,
   221→  );
   222→
   223→  return {
   224→    availableFilters: await fetchAvailableTaskLogFilters(projectIds, projects, hiddenTaskTypes),
   225→    projects,
   226→    tasks: enrichedTasks,
   227→    total: count || 0,
   228→  };
   229→}
   230→
```

     2→import { Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
     3→import { Label } from '@/shared/components/ui/primitives/label';
     4→import { FileInputEmptyState } from '@/shared/components/FileInput/FileInputEmptyState';
     5→import { FileInputSelectedFiles } from '@/shared/components/FileInput/FileInputSelectedFiles';
     6→import { useFileInputController } from '@/shared/components/FileInput/useFileInputController';
     7→
     8→interface FileInputProps {
     9→  onFileChange: (files: File[]) => void;
    10→  onFileRemove?: () => void;
    11→  acceptTypes?: ('image' | 'video')[];
    12→  label?: string;
    13→  currentFilePreviewUrl?: string | null;
    14→  currentFileName?: string | null;
    15→  className?: string;
    16→  disabled?: boolean;
    17→  multiple?: boolean;
    18→  /** Hide the "X files selected" summary header */
    19→  suppressSelectionSummary?: boolean;
    20→  /** Hide the "Remove All Files" action */
    21→  suppressRemoveAll?: boolean;
    22→  /** Show a loader immediately on single-file selection to avoid mid-state flicker */
    23→  showLoaderDuringSingleSelection?: boolean;
    24→  /** Customize loader duration (ms) for single selection */
    25→  loaderDurationMs?: number;
    26→  /** Force loader regardless of internal selection-loading state */
    27→  forceLoading?: boolean;
    28→  /** Hide the "Accepted: image, video" text */
    29→  suppressAcceptedTypes?: boolean;
    30→}
    31→
    32→const FileInput: React.FC<FileInputProps> = ({
    33→  onFileChange,
    34→  onFileRemove,
    35→  acceptTypes = ['image'],
    36→  label = 'Input File(s)',
    37→  currentFilePreviewUrl,
    38→  currentFileName,
    39→  className = '',
    40→  disabled = false,
    41→  multiple = false,
    42→  suppressSelectionSummary = false,
    43→  suppressRemoveAll = false,
    44→  showLoaderDuringSingleSelection = false,
    45→  loaderDurationMs = 600,
    46→  forceLoading = false,
    47→  suppressAcceptedTypes = false,
    48→}) => {
    49→  const controller = useFileInputController({
    50→    onFileChange,
    51→    onFileRemove,
    52→    acceptTypes,
    53→    currentFilePreviewUrl: currentFilePreviewUrl ?? null,
    54→    currentFileName: currentFileName ?? null,
    55→    disabled,
    56→    multiple,
    57→    showLoaderDuringSingleSelection,
    58→    loaderDurationMs,
    59→    forceLoading,
    60→  });
    61→
    62→  return (
    63→    <div className={`space-y-2 ${className}`}>
    64→      {label && <Label htmlFor="file-input-element">{label}</Label>}
    65→      <div
    66→        className={`border-2 border-dashed rounded-md p-6 text-center
    67→                    ${controller.isDraggingOver ? 'border-primary bg-primary/10' : 'border-muted-foreground/30'}
    68→                    ${disabled ? 'cursor-not-allowed bg-muted/50' : 'cursor-pointer hover:border-muted-foreground/50'}`}
    69→        onDragOver={controller.handleDragOver}
    70→        onDragLeave={controller.handleDragLeave}
    71→        onDrop={controller.handleDrop}
    72→        onClick={() => !disabled && controller.fileInputRef.current?.click()}
    73→      >
    74→        <input
    75→          id="file-input-element"
    76→          ref={controller.fileInputRef}
    77→          type="file"
    78→          accept={controller.acceptedMimeTypes}
    79→          onChange={controller.handleInputChange}
    80→          className="hidden"
    81→          disabled={disabled}
    82→          multiple={multiple}
    83→        />
    84→
    85→        {controller.showLoader ? (
    86→          <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-y-2 text-muted-foreground">
    87→            <Loader2 className="h-6 w-6 animate-spin" />
    88→            <p>Processing file...</p>
    89→          </div>
    90→        ) : (
    91→          <>
    92→            {controller.displayFiles.length === 0 && (
    93→              <FileInputEmptyState
    94→                multiple={multiple}
    95→                acceptTypes={acceptTypes}
    96→                suppressAcceptedTypes={suppressAcceptedTypes}
    97→              />
    98→            )}
    99→
   100→            {controller.displayFiles.length > 0 && (
   101→              <FileInputSelectedFiles
   102→                displayFiles={controller.displayFiles}
   103→                displayPreviewUrls={controller.displayPreviewUrls}
   104→                multiple={multiple}
   105→                disabled={disabled}
   106→                suppressSelectionSummary={suppressSelectionSummary}
   107→                suppressRemoveAll={suppressRemoveAll}
   108→                onRemoveAll={controller.handleRemoveAllFiles}
   109→                onRemoveFile={controller.handleRemoveFile}
   110→              />
   111→            )}
   112→          </>
   113→        )}
   114→      </div>
   115→    </div>
   116→  );
   117→};
   118→
   119→export { FileInput };
   120→

tool_result Read

1→import {
     2→  configureVideoForFrameCapture,
     3→  createVideoFrameCaptureElements,
     4→} from './videoFrameCapturePrimitives';
     5→
     6→interface CaptureVideoFrameBlobOptions {
     7→  source: File | string;
     8→  crossOrigin?: string;
     9→  resolveSeekTime: (video: HTMLVideoElement) => number;
    10→  configureCanvas?: (video: HTMLVideoElement, canvas: HTMLCanvasElement) => void;
    11→  onVideoError?: (video: HTMLVideoElement) => Error;
    12→  type?: string;
    13→  quality?: number;
    14→}
    15→
    16→function resolveVideoSource(source: File | string): { url: string; release: () => void } {
    17→  if (typeof source === 'string') {
    18→    return {
    19→      url: source,
    20→      release: () => {},
    21→    };
    22→  }
    23→
    24→  const objectUrl = URL.createObjectURL(source);
    25→  return {
    26→    url: objectUrl,
    27→    release: () => URL.revokeObjectURL(objectUrl),
    28→  };
    29→}
    30→
    31→export function captureVideoFrameBlob(options: CaptureVideoFrameBlobOptions): Promise<Blob> {
    32→  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    33→    const captureElements = createVideoFrameCaptureElements();
    34→    if (!captureElements) {
    35→      reject(new Error('Failed to get canvas context'));
    36→      return;
    37→    }
    38→
    39→    const { video, canvas, ctx } = captureElements;
    40→    const { url, release } = resolveVideoSource(options.source);
    41→    let settled = false;
    42→
    43→    configureVideoForFrameCapture(video, options.crossOrigin);
    44→
    45→    const cleanup = () => {
    46→      if (typeof video.removeEventListener === 'function') {
    47→        video.removeEventListener('loadedmetadata', handleLoadedMetadata);
    48→        video.removeEventListener('seeked', handleSeeked);
    49→        video.removeEventListener('error', handleError);
    50→      }
    51→      video.src = '';
    52→      if (typeof video.remove === 'function') {
    53→        video.remove();
    54→      }
    55→      release();
    56→    };
    57→
    58→    const fail = (error: unknown) => {
    59→      if (settled) return;
    60→      settled = true;
    61→      cleanup();
    62→      reject(error);
    63→    };
    64→
    65→    const handleLoadedMetadata = () => {
    66→      try {
    67→        if (options.configureCanvas) {
    68→          options.configureCanvas(video, canvas);
    69→        } else {
    70→          canvas.width = video.videoWidth;
    71→          canvas.height = video.videoHeight;
    72→        }
    73→
    74→        video.currentTime = Math.max(0, options.resolveSeekTime(video));
    75→      } catch (error) {
    76→        fail(error);
    77→      }
    78→    };
    79→
    80→    const handleSeeked = () => {
    81→      try {
    82→        ctx.drawImage(video, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    83→        canvas.toBlob(
    84→          (blob) => {
    85→            if (!blob) {
    86→              fail(new Error('Failed to create blob from canvas'));
    87→              return;
    88→            }
    89→
    90→            if (settled) {
    91→              cleanup();
    92→              return;
    93→            }
    94→
    95→            settled = true;
    96→            cleanup();
    97→            resolve(blob);
    98→          },
    99→          options.type ?? 'image/jpeg',
   100→          options.quality ?? 0.85,
   101→        );
   102→      } catch (error) {
   103→        fail(error);
   104→      }
   105→    };
   106→
   107→    const handleError = () => {
   108→      fail(options.onVideoError?.(video) ?? new Error('Failed to load video'));
   109→    };
   110→
   111→    video.addEventListener('loadedmetadata', handleLoadedMetadata);
   112→    video.addEventListener('seeked', handleSeeked);
   113→    video.addEventListener('error', handleError);
   114→    video.src = url;
   115→  });
   116→}
   117→
```

     9→interface VisualizationProps {
    10→  gapFrames: number;
    11→  contextFrames: number;

    13→  keepBridgingImages: boolean;

    16→}

    18→export const Visualization: React.FC<VisualizationProps> = ({

    62→        contextFrames={contextFrames}
    63→        replaceMode={replaceMode}

   414→          itemShotWorkflow={itemShotWorkflow}

   420→        />

   424→      {/* Mobile floating bottom bar with pagination and star filter - phones only, not iPad */}

   436→        />

   442→export { MediaGallery };

1→import {
     2→  callUpdateToolSettingsAtomicRpc,
     3→  resolveSettingsScopeTable,
     4→  selectSettingsForScope,
     5→} from '@/shared/lib/toolSettingsWriteRepository';
     6→import {
     7→  enqueueSettingsWrite,
     8→  initializeSettingsWriteQueue,
     9→  type QueuedWrite,
    10→} from '@/shared/lib/settingsWriteQueue';
    11→import { deepMerge } from '@/shared/lib/utils/deepEqual';
    12→import { isCancellationError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/errorUtils';
    13→import { ToolSettingsError } from '@/shared/lib/toolSettingsService';
    14→
    15→export type SettingsScope = 'user' | 'project' | 'shot';
    16→type SettingsWriteMode = 'debounced' | 'immediate';
    17→
    18→interface UpdateToolSettingsParams {
    19→  scope: SettingsScope;
    20→  id: string;
    21→  toolId: string;
    22→  patch: unknown;
    23→}
    24→
    25→interface AbortSignalCapable<T> {
    26→  abortSignal?: (signal: AbortSignal) => T;
    27→}
    28→
    29→function isAbortSignal(value: unknown): value is AbortSignal {
    30→  return !!value
    31→    && typeof value === 'object'
    32→    && 'aborted' in value
    33→    && typeof (value as AbortSignal).addEventListener === 'function';
    34→}
    35→
    36→function isSettingsWriteMode(value: unknown): value is SettingsWriteMode {
    37→  return value === 'debounced' || value === 'immediate';
    38→}
    39→
    40→function maybeAttachAbortSignal<T>(query: T, signal?: AbortSignal): T {
    41→  if (!signal) return query;
    42→
    43→  const candidate = query as T & AbortSignalCapable<T>;
    44→  if (typeof candidate.abortSignal === 'function') {
    45→    return candidate.abortSignal(signal);
    46→  }
    47→
    48→  return query;
    49→}
    50→
    51→function throwIfAborted(signal?: AbortSignal): void {
    52→  if (signal?.aborted) {
    53→    throw new ToolSettingsError('cancelled', 'Request was cancelled', {
    54→      recoverable: true,
    55→      cause: signal.reason,
    56→    });
    57→  }
    58→}
    59→
    60→async function fetchSettingsForScope(
    61→  scope: SettingsScope,
    62→  id: string,
    63→  signal?: AbortSignal,
    64→) {
    65→  throwIfAborted(signal);
    66→
    67→  return maybeAttachAbortSignal(selectSettingsForScope(scope, id), signal);
    68→}
    69→
    70→async function rawUpdateToolSettings(write: QueuedWrite): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
    71→  const { scope, entityId: id, toolId, patch, signal } = write;
    72→
    73→  try {
    74→    if (scope !== 'user' && scope !== 'project' && scope !== 'shot') {
    75→      throw new ToolSettingsError(
    76→        'invalid_scope_identifier',
    77→        `Invalid scope: ${scope}`,
    78→      );
    79→    }
    80→
    81→    const tableName = resolveSettingsScopeTable(scope);
    82→    const { data: currentEntity, error: fetchError } = await fetchSettingsForScope(scope, id, signal);
    83→
    84→    if (fetchError) {
    85→      const errorMessage = fetchError.message || '';
    86→      if (
    87→        errorMessage.includes('ERR_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES')
    88→        || errorMessage.includes('Failed to fetch')
    89→        || fetchError.code === 'ERR_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES'
    90→      ) {
    91→        throw new ToolSettingsError(
    92→          'network',
    93→          `Network exhaustion: ${errorMessage}`,
    94→          { recoverable: true, cause: fetchError },
    95→        );
    96→      }
    97→      throw new ToolSettingsError(
    98→        'scope_fetch_failed',
    99→        `Failed to fetch current ${scope} settings: ${errorMessage}`,
   100→        { recoverable: true, cause: fetchError },
   101→      );
   102→    }
   103→
   104→    const currentSettings = (currentEntity?.settings as Record<string, unknown>) ?? {};
   105→    const currentToolSettings = (currentSettings[toolId] as Record<string, unknown>) ?? {};
   106→    const updatedToolSettings = deepMerge({}, currentToolSettings, patch);
   107→
   108→    throwIfAborted(signal);
   109→    const { error: rpcError } = await maybeAttachAbortSignal(
   110→      callUpdateToolSettingsAtomicRpc(
   111→        tableName,
   112→        id,
   113→        toolId,
   114→        updatedToolSettings,
   115→      ),
   116→      signal,
   117→    );
   118→
   119→    if (rpcError) {
   120→      throw new ToolSettingsError(
   121→        'scope_fetch_failed',
   122→        `Failed to update ${scope} settings: ${rpcError.message}`,
   123→        { recoverable: true, cause: rpcError },
   124→      );
   125→    }
   126→
   127→    return updatedToolSettings;
   128→  } catch (error: unknown) {
   129→    if (isCancellationError(error)) {
   130→      throw new ToolSettingsError('cancelled', 'Request was cancelled', {
   131→        recoverable: true,
   132→        cause: error,
   133→      });
   134→    }
   135→
   136→    throw error;
   137→  }
   138→}
   139→
   140→export function initializeToolSettingsWriteRuntime(): void {
   141→  initializeSettingsWriteQueue(rawUpdateToolSettings);
   142→}
   143→
   144→export function updateToolSettingsSupabase(
   145→  params: UpdateToolSettingsParams,
   146→  mode?: SettingsWriteMode,
   147→): Promise<Record<string, unknown>>;
   148→export function updateToolSettingsSupabase(
   149→  params: UpdateToolSettingsParams,
   150→  signal?: AbortSignal,
   151→  mode?: SettingsWriteMode,
   152→): Promise<Record<string, unknown>>;
   153→export function updateToolSettingsSupabase(
   154→  params: UpdateToolSettingsParams,
   155→  signalOrMode?: AbortSignal | SettingsWriteMode,
   156→  maybeMode: SettingsWriteMode = 'debounced',
   157→): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
   158→  const { scope, id, toolId, patch } = params;
   159→  const signal = isAbortSignal(signalOrMode) ? signalOrMode : undefined;
   160→  const mode = isSettingsWriteMode(signalOrMode) ? signalOrMode : maybeMode;
   161→
   162→  return enqueueSettingsWrite({
   163→    scope,
   164→    entityId: id,
   165→    toolId,
   166→    patch: patch as Record<string, unknown>,
   167→    ...(signal ? { signal } : {}),
   168→  }, mode, rawUpdateToolSettings) as Promise<Record<string, unknown>>;
   169→}
   170→

   210→  const imageKey = image.id || `image-${actualDisplayUrl}-${index}`;
   211→  const isPlaceholder = !image.id && actualDisplayUrl === "/placeholder.svg";
   212→  const currentTargetShotName = selectedShotIdLocal ? simplifiedShotOptions.find(s => s.id === selectedShotIdLocal)?.name : undefined;
   213→  const handleDragStart = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {
   214→    if (isMobile) {
   215→      e.preventDefault();
   216→      return;
   217→    }
   218→    setIsDragging(true);
   219→    setGenerationDragData(e, {
   220→      generationId: image.id,
   221→      imageUrl: image.url,
   222→      thumbUrl: image.thumbUrl,
   223→      metadata: image.metadata
   224→    });
   225→    const cleanup = createDragPreview(e);
   226→    if (cleanup) {
   227→      setTimeout(cleanup, 0);
   228→    }
   229→  }, [image, isMobile, setIsDragging]);
   230→  const handleDragEnd = useCallback(() => {
   231→    setIsDragging(false);
   232→  }, [setIsDragging]);
   233→  const { handleTouchStart, handleInteraction } = useItemInteraction({
   234→    image,
   235→    isMobile,
   236→    mobileActiveImageId,
   237→    enableSingleClick,
   238→    onImageClick,
   239→    onMobileTap,
   240→  });
   241→  const {
   242→    isAlreadyPositionedInSelectedShot,
   243→    isAlreadyAssociatedWithoutPosition,
   244→    shouldShowAddWithoutPositionButton,
   245→  } = useShotPositionChecks({
   246→    image,
   247→    selectedShotIdLocal,
   248→    currentViewingShotId,
   249→    optimisticPositionedIds,
   250→    optimisticUnpositionedIds,
   251→    onAddToLastShotWithoutPosition,
   252→    showTickForImageId,
   253→    addingToShotImageId,
   254→  });
   255→  const aspectRatioPadding = resolveAspectRatioPadding(image, projectAspectRatio);
   256→  const minHeight = '120px'; // Minimum height for very small images
   257→  if (isPlaceholder) {
   258→    return (
   259→      <div
   260→        key={imageKey}
   261→        className="border rounded-lg overflow-hidden bg-muted animate-pulse"
   262→      >
   263→        <div style={{ paddingBottom: aspectRatioPadding }} className="relative">
   264→          <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
   265→            <Eye className="h-12 w-12 text-muted-foreground opacity-30" />
   266→          </div>
   267→        </div>
   268→      </div>
   269→    );
   270→  }
   271→  const imageContent = (
   272→    <div
   273→        className={`border rounded-lg overflow-hidden hover:shadow-md transition-all duration-200 relative group bg-card ${
   274→          isDragging ? 'opacity-50 scale-75' : ''
   275→        } ${!isMobile ? 'cursor-grab active:cursor-grabbing' : ''}`}
   276→        draggable={!isMobile}
   277→        onDragStart={handleDragStart}
   278→        onDragEnd={handleDragEnd}
   279→        onMouseEnter={handleMouseEnter}
   280→        data-tour={dataTour}
   281→        onTouchStart={isMobile && !enableSingleClick && !isVideoContent ? handleTouchStart : undefined}
   282→        onTouchEnd={isMobile && !enableSingleClick && !isVideoContent ? handleInteraction : undefined}
   283→    >
   284→      {/* Image layer */}
   285→      <div
   286→        style={{
   287→          paddingBottom: aspectRatioPadding,
   288→          minHeight: minHeight
   289→        }}
   290→        className="relative bg-muted/50"
   291→      >
   292→          {isVideoContent ? (
   293→            <VideoContent
   294→              image={image}
   295→              stableDisplayUrl={stableDisplayUrl}
   296→              stableVideoUrl={stableVideoUrl}
   297→              actualSrc={actualSrc}
   298→              shouldLoad={shouldLoad}
   299→              imageLoaded={imageLoaded}
   300→              videosAsThumbnails={videosAsThumbnails}
   301→              isMobile={isMobile}
   302→              enableSingleClick={enableSingleClick}
   303→              onImageClick={onImageClick}
   304→              onOpenLightbox={onOpenLightbox}
   305→              onTouchStart={handleTouchStart}
   306→              onTouchEnd={handleInteraction}
   307→              onVideoError={handleImageError}
   308→              onLoadStart={() => setImageLoading(true)}
   309→              onLoadedData={handleImageLoad}
   310→            />
   311→          ) : (
   312→            <ImageContent
   313→              image={image}
   314→              index={index}
   315→              actualSrc={actualSrc}
   316→              imageLoaded={imageLoaded}
   317→              imageLoadError={imageLoadError}
   318→              progressiveEnabled={progressiveEnabled}
   319→              isThumbShowing={isThumbShowing}
   320→              isFullLoaded={isFullLoaded}
   321→              progressiveRef={progressiveRef}
   322→              isMobile={isMobile}
   323→              enableSingleClick={enableSingleClick}
   324→              onImageClick={onImageClick}
   325→              onOpenLightbox={onOpenLightbox}
   326→              onImageLoad={handleImageLoad}
   327→              onImageError={handleImageError}
   328→              onRetry={retryImageLoad}
   329→              setImageLoading={setImageLoading}
   330→            />
   331→          )}
   332→      </div>
   333→      {/* Overlay layer — single container above the image for all UI controls */}
   334→      {image.id && (
   335→      <div className="absolute inset-0 z-10 pointer-events-none [&>*]:pointer-events-auto">
   336→          <ItemShotBadges
   337→            image={image}
   338→            isVideoContent={isVideoContent}
   339→            simplifiedShotOptions={simplifiedShotOptions}
   340→            onMarkAllVariantsViewed={handleMarkAllVariantsViewed}
   341→            onNavigateToShot={(shotId) => {
   342→              const targetShot = simplifiedShotOptions.find((shot) => shot.id === shotId);
   343→              if (targetShot) {
   344→                navigateToShot(targetShot as Shot, { scrollToTop: true });
   345→              }
   346→            }}
   347→          />
   348→          {/* Add to Shot UI - Top Left (for non-video content) */}
   349→          {showAddToShot && simplifiedShotOptions.length > 0 && onAddToLastShot && (
   350→            <ShotActions
   351→              image={image}
   352→              selector={{
   353→                selectedShotId: selectedShotIdLocal,
   354→                simplifiedShotOptions,
   355→                isShotSelectorOpen,
   356→                setIsShotSelectorOpen,
   357→                setSelectedShotIdLocal,
   358→                setLastAffectedShotId,
   359→              }}
   360→              status={{
   361→                isMobile,
   362→                isVideoContent,
   363→                addingToShotImageId,
   364→                addingToShotWithoutPositionImageId: addingToShotWithoutPositionImageId ?? null,
   365→                showTickForImageId,
   366→                isAlreadyPositionedInSelectedShot,
   367→                isAlreadyAssociatedWithoutPosition,
   368→                shouldShowAddWithoutPositionButton,
   369→                currentTargetShotName,
   370→              }}
   371→              quickCreate={{
   372→                quickCreateSuccess,
   373→                handleQuickCreateAndAdd,
   374→                handleVisitCreatedShot,
   375→              }}
   376→              actions={{
   377→                onCreateShot,
   378→                onNavigateToShot: (shot) => navigateToShot(shot, { scrollToTop: true }),
   379→                onAddToShot: addToShot,
   380→                onAddToShotWithoutPosition: addToShotWithoutPosition,
   381→              }}
   382→            />
   383→          )}
   384→          {/* Timestamp - Top Right (hides on hover for images, stays visible for videos) */}
   385→          <TimeStamp
   386→            createdAt={image.createdAt}
   387→            position="top-right"
   388→            showOnHover={false}
   389→            hideOnHover={!isVideoContent}
   390→            className=""
   391→          />
   392→          <ItemMetadataBar
   393→            image={image}
   394→            isVideoContent={isVideoContent}
   395→            isMobile={isMobile}
   396→            taskData={taskData}
   397→            inputImages={inputImages}
   398→            shouldShowMetadata={shouldShowMetadata}
   399→            shouldShowTaskDetails={shouldShowTaskDetails}
   400→            setIsInfoOpen={setIsInfoOpen}
   401→            showShare={showShare}
   402→            taskId={taskId}
   403→            handleShare={handleShare}
   404→            isCreatingShare={isCreatingShare}
   405→            shareCopied={shareCopied}
   406→            shareSlug={shareSlug}
   407→            onMarkAllVariantsViewed={handleMarkAllVariantsViewed}
   408→          />
   409→          <ActionButtons
   410→            image={image}
   411→            localStarred={localStarred}
   412→            isTogglingStar={isTogglingStar}
   413→            isDeleting={isCurrentDeleting}
   414→            showStar={showStar}
   415→            showEdit={showEdit}
   416→            showDelete={showDelete}
   417→            onToggleStar={onToggleStar}
   418→            setIsTogglingStar={setIsTogglingStar}
   419→            setLocalStarred={setLocalStarred}
   420→            onOpenLightbox={onOpenLightbox}
   421→            onDelete={onDelete}
   422→          />
   423→      </div>)
   424→      }
   425→    </div>
   426→  );
   427→  return isMobile ? (
   428→    <React.Fragment key={imageKey}>
   429→      {imageContent}
   430→      {onCreateShot && (
   431→        <CreateShotModal
   432→          isOpen={isCreateShotModalOpen}
   433→          onClose={() => setIsCreateShotModalOpen(false)}
   434→          onSubmit={handleCreateShot}
   435→          isLoading={isCreatingShot}
   436→          projectId={selectedProjectId ?? undefined}
   437→        />
   438→      )}
   439→    </React.Fragment>
   440→  ) : (
   441→    <DraggableImage key={`draggable-${imageKey}`} image={image} onDoubleClick={() => onOpenLightbox(image)}>
   442→      {imageContent}
   443→      {onCreateShot && (
   444→        <CreateShotModal
   445→          isOpen={isCreateShotModalOpen}
   446→          onClose={() => setIsCreateShotModalOpen(false)}
   447→          onSubmit={handleCreateShot}
   448→          isLoading={isCreatingShot}
   449→          projectId={selectedProjectId ?? undefined}
   450→        />
   451→      )}
   452→    </DraggableImage>
   453→  );
   454→};
   455→

}

     3→import { useImageTickFeedback } from '../useImageTickFeedback';
     4→
     5→describe('useImageTickFeedback', () => {

    10→  afterEach(() => {
    11→    vi.useRealTimers();
    12→  });
    13→
    14→  it('clears the primary tick id after 3 seconds', () => {
    15→    const { result } = renderHook(() => useImageTickFeedback());
    16→
    17→    act(() => {
    18→      result.current.setShowTickForImageId('img-1');

    28→      vi.advanceTimersByTime(1);
    29→    });
    30→    expect(result.current.showTickForImageId).toBeNull();
    31→  });
    32→
    33→  it('clears the secondary tick id independently', () => {
    34→    const { result } = renderHook(() => useImageTickFeedback());
    35→
    36→    act(() => {
    37→      result.current.setShowTickForImageId('img-1');
    38→      result.current.setShowTickForSecondaryImageId('img-2');

  "new_string": "import { setGenerationDragData, createDragPreview } from '@/shared/lib/dnd/dragDrop';\nimport type { GeneratedImageWithMetadata } from '@/shared/components/MediaGallery/types';\nimport { parseRatio } from '@/shared/lib/media/aspectRatios';\n\nconst MIN_PADDING = 60;\nconst MAX_PADDING = 200;\n\nfunction clampPadding(value: number): number {\n  return Math.min(Math.max(value, MIN_PADDING), MAX_PADDING);\n}\n\nfunction extractResolutionFromMetadata(image: GeneratedImageWithMetadata): { width?: number; height?: number } {\n  let width = image.metadata?.width;\n  let height = image.metadata?.height;\n\n  if (width && height) {\n    return { width, height };\n  }\n\n  const resolution = image.metadata?.originalParams?.orchestrator_details?.resolution;\n  if (resolution && typeof resolution === 'string' && resolution.includes('x')) {\n    const [parsedWidth, parsedHeight] = resolution.split('x').map(Number);\n    if (!isNaN(parsedWidth) && !isNaN(parsedHeight)) {\n      width = parsedWidth;\n      height = parsedHeight;\n    }\n  }\n\n  return { width, height };\n}\n\nfunction resolveAspectRatioPadding(\n  image: GeneratedImageWithMetadata,\n  projectAspectRatio?: string,\n): string {\n  if (projectAspectRatio) {\n    const ratio = parseRatio(projectAspectRatio);\n    if (!isNaN(ratio)) {\n      const calculatedPadding = (1 / ratio) * 100;\n      return `${clampPadding(calculatedPadding)}%`;\n    }\n  }\n\n  const { width, height } = extractResolutionFromMetadata(image);\n  if (width && height) {\n    return `${clampPadding((height / width) * 100)}%`;\n  }\n\n  return '100%';\n}"
}

This test is now problematic. The tests call `resolveSettingsScopeTable` and `selectSettingsForScope` as mocks, but these are now inlined private functions in the service module - they can't be mocked separately. The test at line 82 asserts `mocks.resolveSettingsScopeTable` was called, but `resolveSettingsScopeTable` is now a private function that won't go through the mock.

The mock structure needs to change since the functions are inlined. Now:
- `resolveSettingsScopeTable` is a pure function that needs no mock (it just maps scope to table name)
- `selectSettingsForScope` calls `getSupabaseClient().from(tableName).select().eq().single()` - so we need to mock the supabase client
- `callUpdateToolSettingsAtomicRpc` calls `getSupabaseClient().rpc()` - also needs supabase client mock

The current mock of `@/integrations/supabase/client` I set up is too simplistic - it doesn't chain `.select().eq().single()`. Let me fix this properly.

Two test files were deleted in commit 6fd933f83 and need replacement tests covering the same behavior through the current public API.

## Test 1: Video Lightbox Render Model

**Deleted test:** `src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/videoLightbox/renderModelBuilders.test.tsx` (191 lines)
**What it tested:** Internal builder functions (`__internal.buildVideoLightboxVariantsModel`, `__internal.buildVideoLightboxStateValue`, `__internal.buildVideoEditPanelModel`, `__internal.buildVideoControlsPanelContent`) that mapped input data to model shapes.
**Current code:** These builders are now inline `useMemo` blocks in `src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/videoLightbox/useVideoLightboxRenderModel.tsx`

The key behavioral tests were:
1. Variants model maps `pendingTaskCount` from `editModel.variantBadges.pendingTaskCount`
2. `onMarkAllViewed` is correctly wired to `editModel.variantBadges.handleMarkAllViewed`
3. State value maps `effectiveVideoUrl` from `sharedState.effectiveMedia.videoUrl`
4. Navigation `hasNext` is correctly mapped from mode model
5. Controls panel renders `VideoEditPanel` when `isInVideoEditMode` is true and `videoEditSubMode` is set
6. Controls panel renders `InfoPanel` otherwise

Write a new test file at `src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/videoLightbox/useVideoLightboxRenderModel.test.tsx`.

The hook `useVideoLightboxRenderModel` takes 5 arguments:
- props: VideoLightboxPropsWithMedia
- modeModel: VideoLightboxModeModel
- env: VideoLightboxEnvironment
- sharedState: VideoLightboxSharedStateModel
- editModel: VideoLightboxEditModel

It internally calls:
- `useVideoLightboxActions` (mock this module)
- `buildVideoLightboxLayoutState` (imported from `../../model/buildVideoLightboxLayoutState` - mock this)

Read the source file to understand the types and create appropriate mocks. Use `renderHook` to test the hook.

## Test 2: Apply Settings Handler

**Deleted test:** `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/controllers/useShotEditorApplySettingsModel.test.tsx` (155 lines)
**What it tested:** An adapter that mapped editor settings slices into `useApplySettingsHandler`'s contract. Key behaviors:
- Correct mapping of `setMotionMode` for `onAdvancedModeChange`
- Result delegates to `useApplySettingsHandler`

**Current code:** The adapter was inlined directly into `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/useShotEditorController.ts` (lines 312-365). The key testable behavior from the old test was:
- `onAdvancedModeChange(true)` calls `setMotionMode('advanced')`
- `onAdvancedModeChange(false)` calls `setMotionMode('basic')`

Since the controller is too large to unit test directly, write a test for `useApplySettingsHandler` at `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/hooks/actions/useApplySettingsHandler.test.ts` that verifies:
1. The handler returns a stable callback
2. The handler calls `applySettingsFromTask` with the latest handler state (via ref)
3. The handler shows an error toast when `simpleFilteredImages` has missing IDs and `replaceImages` is true

Read the source at `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/hooks/actions/useApplySettingsHandler.ts` for the full implementation.

1→/**
     2→ * Stable Apply Settings Handler Hook
     3→ *
     4→ * Provides a stable callback for applying settings from tasks without
     5→ * recreating on every render.
     6→ */
     7→
     8→import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
     9→import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
    10→import * as ApplySettingsService from '../../services/applySettingsService';
    11→import type { GenerationRow, Shot } from '@/domains/generation/types';
    12→import type { LoraModel } from '@/domains/lora/types/lora';
    13→import { enqueueGenerationsInvalidation } from '@/shared/hooks/invalidation/useGenerationInvalidation';
    14→import type { AddImageToShotVariables } from '@/shared/hooks/shots/addImageToShotHelpers';
    15→import { normalizeAndPresentError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/runtimeError';
    16→
    17→interface ApplySettingsHandlerCore {
    18→  projectId: string;
    19→  selectedShot: Shot | undefined;
    20→  simpleFilteredImages: GenerationRow[];
    21→}
    22→
    23→export interface ApplySettingsHandlerContexts {
    24→  model: ApplySettingsService.ApplyModelContext;
    25→  prompts: ApplySettingsService.ApplyPromptContext;
    26→  generation: ApplySettingsService.ApplyGenerationContext;
    27→  modes: ApplySettingsService.ApplyModeContext;
    28→  advanced: ApplySettingsService.ApplyAdvancedContext;
    29→  textAddons: ApplySettingsService.ApplyTextAddonContext;
    30→  motion: ApplySettingsService.ApplyMotionContext;
    31→  loras: ApplySettingsService.ApplyLoraContext;
    32→  structureVideo: ApplySettingsService.ApplyStructureVideoContext;
    33→}
    34→
    35→interface ApplySettingsHandlerMutations {
    36→  addImageToShotMutation: {
    37→    mutateAsync: (params: AddImageToShotVariables) => Promise<unknown>;
    38→  };
    39→  removeImageFromShotMutation: {
    40→    mutateAsync: (params: {
    41→      shotId: string;
    42→      shotGenerationId: string;
    43→      projectId: string;
    44→      shiftItems?: { id: string; newFrame: number }[];
    45→    }) => Promise<unknown>;
    46→  };
    47→  loadPositions: (opts?: { silent?: boolean; reason?: string }) => void | Promise<void>;
    48→}
    49→
    50→interface ApplySettingsHandlerInput {
    51→  core: ApplySettingsHandlerCore;
    52→  contexts: ApplySettingsHandlerContexts;
    53→  mutations: ApplySettingsHandlerMutations;
    54→}
    55→
    56→async function fetchTaskData(taskId: string) {
    57→  return ApplySettingsService.fetchTask(taskId);
    58→}
    59→
    60→async function applySettingsFromTask(
    61→  taskId: string,
    62→  replaceImages: boolean,
    63→  inputImages: string[],
    64→  handlerState: ApplySettingsHandlerInput,
    65→  queryClient: ReturnType<typeof useQueryClient>,
    66→) {
    67→  const taskLookup = await fetchTaskData(taskId);
    68→  if (taskLookup.status === 'missing') {
    69→    normalizeAndPresentError(new Error('Task not found'), {
    70→      context: 'useApplySettingsHandler',
    71→      toastTitle: 'Failed to apply settings from task',
    72→      showToast: false,
    73→      logData: { taskId },
    74→    });
    75→    return;
    76→  }
    77→
    78→  const settings = ApplySettingsService.extractSettings(taskLookup.taskData);
    79→
    80→  await ApplySettingsService.replaceImagesIfRequested(
    81→    settings.generation,
    82→    settings.images,
    83→    replaceImages,
    84→    inputImages,
    85→    handlerState.core.selectedShot ?? null,
    86→    handlerState.core.projectId,
    87→    handlerState.core.simpleFilteredImages,
    88→    handlerState.mutations.addImageToShotMutation,
    89→    handlerState.mutations.removeImageFromShotMutation,
    90→  );
    91→
    92→  await ApplySettingsService.applyModelSettings(settings.generation, handlerState.contexts.model);
    93→  await ApplySettingsService.applyPromptSettings(settings.prompts, handlerState.contexts.prompts);
    94→  await ApplySettingsService.applyGenerationSettings(settings.generation, handlerState.contexts.generation);
    95→  await ApplySettingsService.applyModeSettings(settings.modes, handlerState.contexts.modes);
    96→  await ApplySettingsService.applyAdvancedModeSettings(settings.advanced, handlerState.contexts.advanced);
    97→  await ApplySettingsService.applyTextPromptAddons(settings.textAddons, handlerState.contexts.textAddons);
    98→  await ApplySettingsService.applyMotionSettings(
    99→    { ...settings.motion, advancedMode: settings.modes.advancedMode },
   100→    handlerState.contexts.motion,
   101→  );
   102→  await ApplySettingsService.applyLoRAs(
   103→    { ...settings.loras, advancedMode: settings.modes.advancedMode },
   104→    handlerState.contexts.loras,
   105→  );
   106→  await ApplySettingsService.applyStructureVideo(settings.structure, handlerState.contexts.structureVideo);
   107→
   108→  if (handlerState.core.selectedShot?.id) {
   109→    enqueueGenerationsInvalidation(queryClient, handlerState.core.selectedShot.id, {
   110→      reason: 'apply-settings-force-reload',
   111→      scope: 'all',
   112→      delayMs: 200,
   113→    });
   114→  }
   115→
   116→  await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 200));
   117→  await Promise.resolve(handlerState.mutations.loadPositions({ silent: true }));
   118→}
   119→
   120→export function useApplySettingsHandler(handlerState: ApplySettingsHandlerInput) {
   121→  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
   122→  const handlerStateRef = useRef(handlerState);
   123→
   124→  useEffect(() => {
   125→    handlerStateRef.current = handlerState;
   126→  }, [handlerState]);
   127→
   128→  return useCallback(async (taskId: string, replaceImages: boolean, inputImages: string[]) => {
   129→    const latestHandlerState = handlerStateRef.current;
   130→    const hasMissingIds = latestHandlerState.core.simpleFilteredImages.some((img) => !img.id);
   131→    if (hasMissingIds && replaceImages) {
   132→      normalizeAndPresentError(new Error('Loading shot data... please try again in a moment.'), {
   133→        context: 'useApplySettingsHandler',
   134→        toastTitle: 'Loading shot data',
   135→        showToast: true,
   136→      });
   137→      return;
   138→    }
   139→
   140→    try {
   141→      await applySettingsFromTask(taskId, replaceImages, inputImages, latestHandlerState, queryClient);
   142→    } catch (error) {
   143→      normalizeAndPresentError(error, {
   144→        context: 'useApplySettingsHandler',
   145→        toastTitle: 'Failed to apply settings from task',
   146→      });
   147→    }
   148→  }, [queryClient]);
   149→}
   150→

    23→export function buildVideoLightboxLayoutState(
    24→  input: BuildVideoLightboxLayoutStateInput,
    25→): VideoLightboxLayoutState {

    28→

    30→    || ((input.showTaskDetails || input.isAnyVideoEditMode || (input.isSegmentSlotMode && input.hasSegmentVideo)) && input.isMobile);

50→export function useVideoLightboxMode(options: VideoLightboxModeOptions) {
    51→  const {
    52→    segmentSlotMode,
    53→    navigation,
    54→  } = options;
    55→  const hasNextProp = navigation?.hasNext ?? false;
    56→  const hasPreviousProp = navigation?.hasPrevious ?? false;
    57→  const onNext = navigation?.onNext;
    58→  const onPrevious = navigation?.onPrevious;
    59→
    60→  const isSegmentSlotMode = !!segmentSlotMode;
    61→  const hasSegmentVideo = isSegmentSlotMode && !!segmentSlotMode.segmentVideo;
    62→  const isFormOnlyMode = isSegmentSlotMode && !hasSegmentVideo;
    63→
    64→  const hasNext = isSegmentSlotMode
    65→    ? segmentSlotMode.currentIndex < segmentSlotMode.totalPairs - 1
    66→    : hasNextProp;
    67→
    68→  const hasPrevious = isSegmentSlotMode
    69→    ? segmentSlotMode.currentIndex > 0
    70→    : hasPreviousProp;
    71→
    72→  const handleSlotNavNext = useCallback(() => {
    73→    if (isSegmentSlotMode) {
    74→      segmentSlotMode.onNavigateToPair(segmentSlotMode.currentIndex + 1);
    75→      return;
    76→    }
    77→    if (onNext) {
    78→      onNext();
    79→    }
    80→  }, [isSegmentSlotMode, onNext, segmentSlotMode]);
    81→
    82→  const handleSlotNavPrev = useCallback(() => {
    83→    if (isSegmentSlotMode) {
    84→      segmentSlotMode.onNavigateToPair(segmentSlotMode.currentIndex - 1);
    85→      return;
    86→    }
    87→    if (onPrevious) {
    88→      onPrevious();
    89→    }
    90→  }, [isSegmentSlotMode, onPrevious, segmentSlotMode]);
    91→
    92→  return {
    93→    isSegmentSlotMode,
    94→    hasSegmentVideo,
    95→    isFormOnlyMode,
    96→    hasNext,
    97→    hasPrevious,
    98→    handleSlotNavNext,
    99→    handleSlotNavPrev,
   100→  };
   101→}

expect(result.current).toBe(firstCallback);\n  });\n\n  it('calls applySettingsFromTask with the latest handler state via ref', async () => {\n    const initialState = createHandlerState();\n    const updatedContexts = createContexts();\n    updatedContexts.model = { updated: true } as never;\n    const updatedState = createHandlerState({ contexts: updatedContexts });\n\n    const { result, rerender } = renderHook(\n      ({ state }) => useApplySettingsHandler(state),\n      { initialProps: { state: initialState } },\n    );\n\n    // Update the state (simulates a re-render with new props)\n    rerender({ state: updatedState });\n\n    await act(async () => {\n      await result.current('task-42', false, ['img-a']);\n    });\n\n    // Should have fetched the task\n    expect(mocks.fetchTask).toHaveBeenCalledWith('task-42');\n\n    // Should apply model settings with the UPDATED contexts (from the ref)\n    expect(mocks.applyModelSettings).toHaveBeenCalledWith(\n handlerState = createHandlerState({\n      core: {\n        projectId: 'project-1',\n        selectedShot: { id: 'shot-1' },\n        simpleFilteredImages: [{ id: 'img-1' }, { id: undefined }],\n {\n      await result.current('task-99', false, []);\n    });\n\n    // Should NOT show the loading toast - missing IDs only matter when replaceImages is true\n the task\n    expect(mocks.fetchTask).toHaveBeenCalledWith('task-99');\n  });\n\n  it('catches errors from applySettingsFromTask and presents them', async () => {\n    const taskError = new > AGENT

The issue is that in Test 1, I'm importing `InfoPanel` and `VideoEditPanel` directly, which triggers their transitive imports. The old test used `__internal` exports that were just builder functions, not component imports. Since the hook now renders JSX with these components inline, I need to mock those components instead of importing them.

Both test files pass. All 12 tests (7 + 5) pass successfully.

Here is a summary of what was created:

**Test 1: Video Lightbox Render Model** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/videoLightbox/useVideoLightboxRenderModel.test.tsx`) - 7 tests:
1. Maps `pendingTaskCount` from `editModel.variantBadges`
2. Wires `onMarkAllViewed` to `editModel.variantBadges.handleMarkAllViewed` (verifies identity and invocation)
3. Maps `effectiveVideoUrl` from `sharedState.effectiveMedia.videoUrl`
4. Maps navigation `hasNext`/`hasPrevious` from `modeModel`
5. Renders `VideoEditPanel` when `isInVideoEditMode` is true and `videoEditSubMode` is set
6. Renders `InfoPanel` when not in video edit mode
7. Returns `undefined` controlsPanelContent when `showPanel` is false

**Test 2: Apply Settings Handler** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/hooks/actions/useApplySettingsHandler.test.ts`) - 5 tests:
1. Returns a stable callback across re-renders
2. Calls `applySettingsFromTask` with the latest handler state via ref (verifies ref-based state freshness)
3. Shows an error toast when `simpleFilteredImages` has missing IDs and `replaceImages` is true, and does NOT proceed to fetch the task
4. Does not show the error toast when `replaceImages` is false even with missing IDs (guard only applies when replacing)
5. Catches errors from `applySettingsFromTask` and presents them via `normalizeAndPresentError`

    28→interface TravelContractData {

    45→
    46→export interface ResolvedTravelStructureContractData {
    47→  present: boolean;
    48→  structureGuidance?: UnknownRecord;
    49→  structureVideos: ReturnType<typeof resolveTravelStructureState>['structureVideos'];
    50→  primaryStructureVideo: ReturnType<typeof resolvePrimaryStructureVideo>;
    51→}
    52→
    53→function pickFirstDefinedValue(
    54→  reader: ReturnType<typeof createTravelPayloadReader>,
    55→  keys: string[],
    56→  sources: TravelPayloadSource[],
    57→): unknown {
    58→  for (const key of keys) {
    59→    const value = reader.read(key, sources);
    60→    if (value !== undefined) {
    61→      return value;
    62→    }
    63→  }
    64→  return undefined;
    65→}
    66→
    67→function pickRecord(reader: ReturnType<typeof createTravelPayloadReader>, key: string): UnknownRecord | undefined {
    68→  const contractRecord = reader.pickRecord(key, CONTRACT_SOURCES);
    69→  if (contractRecord) {
    70→    return contractRecord;
    71→  }
    72→  const legacyRecord = reader.pickRecord(key, LEGACY_SOURCES);
    73→  return legacyRecord;
    74→}
    75→
    76→/** Field descriptors for structure source: [outputKey, lookupKeys, sources] */
    77→const STRUCTURE_FIELDS: [string, string[], TravelPayloadSource[]][] = [
    78→  ['structure_guidance', ['structure_guidance', 'structureGuidance'], CONTRACT_THEN_LEGACY],
    79→  ['structure_videos', ['structure_videos', 'structureVideos'], CONTRACT_THEN_LEGACY],
    80→  ['structure_video_path', ['structure_video_path', 'structureVideoPath'], LEGACY_SOURCES],
    81→  ['structure_video_treatment', ['structure_video_treatment', 'structureVideoTreatment'], LEGACY_SOURCES],
    82→  ['structure_video_type', ['structure_video_type', 'structure_type', 'structureVideoType', 'structureType'], LEGACY_SOURCES],
    83→  ['structure_video_motion_strength', ['structure_video_motion_strength', 'structureVideoMotionStrength'], LEGACY_SOURCES],
    84→  ['structure_canny_intensity', ['structure_canny_intensity', 'structureCannyIntensity'], LEGACY_SOURCES],
    85→  ['structure_depth_contrast', ['structure_depth_contrast', 'structureDepthContrast'], LEGACY_SOURCES],
    86→  ['uni3c_start_percent', ['uni3c_start_percent', 'uni3cStartPercent'], LEGACY_SOURCES],
    87→  ['uni3c_end_percent', ['uni3c_end_percent', 'uni3cEndPercent'], LEGACY_SOURCES],
    88→  ['use_uni3c', ['use_uni3c', 'useUni3c'], LEGACY_SOURCES],
    89→];
    90→
    91→export function buildTravelStructureSource(
    92→  snapshot: TaskPayloadSnapshot,
    93→): Record<string, unknown> {
    94→  const reader = createTravelPayloadReader(snapshot);
    95→  const result: Record<string, unknown> = {};
    96→
    97→  for (const [outputKey, lookupKeys, sources] of STRUCTURE_FIELDS) {
    98→    const value = pickFirstDefinedValue(reader, lookupKeys, sources);
    99→    if (value !== undefined) {
   100→      result[outputKey] = value;
   101→    }
   102→  }
   103→
   104→  // Fallback: if structure_videos wasn't found, check for videos array inside structure_guidance
   105→  if (result.structure_videos === undefined && result.structure_guidance !== undefined) {
   106→    const guidanceVideos = asRecord(result.structure_guidance)?.videos;
   107→    if (Array.isArray(guidanceVideos)) {
   108→      result.structure_videos = guidanceVideos;
   109→    }
   110→  }
   111→
   112→  return result;
   113→}
   114→
   115→export function readResolvedTravelStructure(
   116→  snapshot: TaskPayloadSnapshot,
   117→): ResolvedTravelStructureContractData {
   118→  const source = buildTravelStructureSource(snapshot);
   119→  const resolved = resolveTravelStructureState(source, {
   120→    defaultEndFrame: 0,
   121→    defaultVideoTreatment: DEFAULT_STRUCTURE_VIDEO.treatment,
   122→    defaultMotionStrength: DEFAULT_STRUCTURE_GUIDANCE_CONTROLS.motionStrength,
   123→    defaultStructureType: DEFAULT_STRUCTURE_GUIDANCE_CONTROLS.structureType,
   124→    defaultUni3cEndPercent: DEFAULT_STRUCTURE_GUIDANCE_CONTROLS.uni3cEndPercent,
   125→  });
   126→
   127→  return {
   128→    present: Object.keys(source).length > 0,
   129→    structureGuidance: resolved.structureGuidance,
   130→    structureVideos: resolved.structureVideos,
   131→    primaryStructureVideo: resolvePrimaryStructureVideo(
   132→      resolved.structureVideos,
   133→      resolved.structureGuidance,
   134→    ),
   135→  };
   136→}
   137→
   138→export function readTravelContractData(snapshot: TaskPayloadSnapshot): TravelContractData {
   139→  const reader = createTravelPayloadReader(snapshot);
   140→  const structureGuidance = pickRecord(reader, 'structure_guidance');
   141→  const phaseConfig = pickRecord(reader, 'phase_config');
   142→
   143→  return {
   144→    inputImages: reader.pickStringArray('input_images', CONTRACT_THEN_LEGACY)
   145→      ?? reader.pickStringArray('input_image_paths_resolved', LEGACY_SOURCES),
   146→    prompt: reader.pickString('prompt', CONTRACT_THEN_LEGACY)

   154→    selectedPhasePresetId: reader.pickString('selected_phase_preset_id', CONTRACT_THEN_LEGACY),
   155→    advancedMode: reader.pickBoolean('advanced_mode', CONTRACT_THEN_LEGACY),

   662→async function handleHuggingFaceUpload(req: Request): Promise<Response> {
   663→  // Handle CORS preflight
   664→  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") {
   665→    return new Response("ok", { headers: corsHeaders });
   666→  }
   667→  if (req.method !== "POST") {
   668→    return createResponse({ error: "Method not allowed" }, 405);

    90→
    91→export function useTaskGeneration({
    92→  media,
    93→  selectedProjectId,
    94→  shotId,
    95→  toolTypeOverride,
    96→  isVideo,
    97→  loras,
    98→  activeVariantId,

   100→  createAsGeneration,
   101→  advancedSettings,
