   107→  strokeOverlayRef,
   108→  handleExitInpaintMode,
   109→  setIsGeneratingInpaint,
   110→  setInpaintGenerateSuccess,
   111→}: UseTaskGenerationProps) {
   112→  // Get actual generation ID (may differ from media.id for shot_generations)
   113→  const actualGenerationId = getGenerationId(media);
   114→  const run = useTaskPlaceholder();
   115→
   116→  /**
   117→   * Unified task generation function
   118→   * Handles both inpaint and annotate modes with mode-specific configuration
   119→   */
   120→  const handleGenerateTask = useCallback(async (taskType: TaskType) => {
   121→    const config = TASK_CONFIGS[taskType];
   122→    const strokes = taskType === 'inpaint' ? inpaintStrokes : annotationStrokes;
   123→
   124→    // Validation
   125→    if (!selectedProjectId || isVideo) {
   126→      toast.error(`Cannot generate ${taskType === 'inpaint' ? 'inpaint' : 'annotated edit'}`);
   127→      return;
   128→    }
   129→
   130→    if (strokes.length === 0) {
   131→      toast.error(config.emptyStrokesError);
   132→      return;
   133→    }
   134→
   135→    if (!inpaintPrompt.trim()) {
   136→      toast.error('Please enter a prompt');
   137→      return;
   138→    }
   139→
   140→    if (!strokeOverlayRef.current) {
   141→      toast.error(config.overlayNotReadyError);
   142→      return;
   143→    }
   144→
   145→    const incomingTaskType = taskType === 'inpaint' ? 'image_inpaint' : 'annotated_image_edit';
   146→
   147→    setIsGeneratingInpaint(true);
   148→    try {
   149→      await run({
   150→        taskType: incomingTaskType,
   151→        label: inpaintPrompt.trim() || 'Editing...',
   152→        context: 'useTaskGeneration',
   153→        toastTitle: config.taskCreationError,
   154→        create: async () => {
   155→          // Export mask directly from Konva
   156→          const maskImageData = strokeOverlayRef.current!.exportMask({ pixelRatio: 1.5 });
   157→
   158→          if (!maskImageData) {
   159→            throw new Error('Failed to export mask from overlay');
   160→          }
   161→
   162→          // Upload mask to storage
   163→          const maskFile = await fetch(maskImageData)
   164→            .then(res => res.blob())
   165→            .then(blob => new File([blob], `${config.fileNamePrefix}_${media.id}_${Date.now()}.png`, { type: 'image/png' }));
   166→
   167→          const maskUrl = await uploadImageToStorage(maskFile);
   168→
   169→          // Get source image URL
   170→          const mediaUrl = getMediaUrl(media) || media.imageUrl;
   171→          const sourceUrl = activeVariantLocation || mediaUrl;
   172→          if (!sourceUrl) {
   173→            throw new Error('Missing source media URL');
   174→          }
   175→          if (!actualGenerationId) {
   176→            throw new Error('Missing generation id');
   177→          }
   178→
   179→          return config.createTask(
   180→            buildMaskedEditTaskParams({
   181→              projectId: selectedProjectId,
   182→              imageUrl: sourceUrl,
   183→              maskUrl,
   184→              prompt: inpaintPrompt,
   185→              numGenerations: inpaintNumGenerations,
   186→              generationId: actualGenerationId ?? undefined,
   187→              shotId,
   188→              toolType: toolTypeOverride,
   189→              loras,
   190→              createAsGeneration,
   191→              sourceVariantId: activeVariantId || undefined,
   192→              hiresFix: convertToHiresFixApiParams(advancedSettings),
   193→              qwenEditModel,
   194→            }),
   195→          );
   196→        },
   197→        onSuccess: () => {
   198→          // Show success state
   199→          setInpaintGenerateSuccess(true);
   200→
   201→          // Wait 1 second to show success, then exit
   202→          setTimeout(() => {
   203→            setInpaintGenerateSuccess(false);
   204→            handleExitInpaintMode();
   205→          }, 1000);
   206→        },
   207→      });
   208→    } finally {
   209→      setIsGeneratingInpaint(false);
   210→    }
   211→  }, [
   212→    selectedProjectId, isVideo, inpaintStrokes, annotationStrokes, inpaintPrompt, inpaintNumGenerations,
   213→    media, handleExitInpaintMode, shotId, toolTypeOverride, loras,
   214→    activeVariantLocation, activeVariantId, createAsGeneration, advancedSettings,
   215→    qwenEditModel, strokeOverlayRef, actualGenerationId,
   216→    setIsGeneratingInpaint, setInpaintGenerateSuccess, run
   217→  ]);
   218→
   219→  // Stable callbacks that preserve the original API
   220→  const handleGenerateInpaint = useCallback(() => {
   221→    return handleGenerateTask('inpaint');
   222→  }, [handleGenerateTask]);
   223→
   224→  const handleGenerateAnnotatedEdit = useCallback(() => {
   225→    return handleGenerateTask('annotate');
   226→  }, [handleGenerateTask]);
   227→
   228→  return {
   229→    handleGenerateInpaint,
   230→    handleGenerateAnnotatedEdit,
   231→  };
   232→}
   233→
```

tool_result Read

1→interface VideoFrameCaptureElements {
     2→  video: HTMLVideoElement;
     3→  canvas: HTMLCanvasElement;
     4→  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D;
     5→}
     6→
     7→function createVideoFrameCaptureElements(): VideoFrameCaptureElements | null {
     8→  const video = document.createElement('video');
     9→  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    10→  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    11→
    12→  if (!ctx) {
    13→    return null;
    14→  }
    15→
    16→  return { video, canvas, ctx };
    17→}
    18→
    19→function configureVideoForFrameCapture(video: HTMLVideoElement, crossOrigin?: string): void {
    20→  if (crossOrigin) {
    21→    video.crossOrigin = crossOrigin;
    22→  }
    23→
    24→  video.preload = 'metadata';
    25→  video.muted = true;
    26→  video.playsInline = true;
    27→}
    28→
    29→interface CaptureVideoFrameBlobOptions {
    30→  source: File | string;
    31→  crossOrigin?: string;
    32→  resolveSeekTime: (video: HTMLVideoElement) => number;
    33→  configureCanvas?: (video: HTMLVideoElement, canvas: HTMLCanvasElement) => void;
    34→  onVideoError?: (video: HTMLVideoElement) => Error;
    35→  type?: string;
    36→  quality?: number;
    37→}
    38→
    39→function resolveVideoSource(source: File | string): { url: string; release: () => void } {
    40→  if (typeof source === 'string') {
    41→    return {
    42→      url: source,
    43→      release: () => {},
    44→    };
    45→  }
    46→
    47→  const objectUrl = URL.createObjectURL(source);
    48→  return {
    49→    url: objectUrl,
    50→    release: () => URL.revokeObjectURL(objectUrl),
    51→  };
    52→}
    53→
    54→export function captureVideoFrameBlob(options: CaptureVideoFrameBlobOptions): Promise<Blob> {
    55→  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    56→    const captureElements = createVideoFrameCaptureElements();
    57→    if (!captureElements) {
    58→      reject(new Error('Failed to get canvas context'));
    59→      return;
    60→    }
    61→
    62→    const { video, canvas, ctx } = captureElements;
    63→    const { url, release } = resolveVideoSource(options.source);
    64→    let settled = false;
    65→
    66→    configureVideoForFrameCapture(video, options.crossOrigin);
    67→
    68→    const cleanup = () => {
    69→      if (typeof video.removeEventListener === 'function') {
    70→        video.removeEventListener('loadedmetadata', handleLoadedMetadata);
    71→        video.removeEventListener('seeked', handleSeeked);
    72→        video.removeEventListener('error', handleError);
    73→      }
    74→      video.src = '';
    75→      if (typeof video.remove === 'function') {
    76→        video.remove();
    77→      }
    78→      release();
    79→    };
    80→
    81→    const fail = (error: unknown) => {
    82→      if (settled) return;
    83→      settled = true;
    84→      cleanup();
    85→      reject(error);
    86→    };
    87→
    88→    const handleLoadedMetadata = () => {
    89→      try {
    90→        if (options.configureCanvas) {
    91→          options.configureCanvas(video, canvas);
    92→        } else {
    93→          canvas.width = video.videoWidth;
    94→          canvas.height = video.videoHeight;
    95→        }
    96→
    97→        video.currentTime = Math.max(0, options.resolveSeekTime(video));
    98→      } catch (error) {
    99→        fail(error);
   100→      }
   101→    };
   102→
   103→    const handleSeeked = () => {
   104→      try {
   105→        ctx.drawImage(video, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
   106→        canvas.toBlob(
   107→          (blob) => {
   108→            if (!blob) {
   109→              fail(new Error('Failed to create blob from canvas'));
   110→              return;
   111→            }
   112→
   113→            if (settled) {
   114→              cleanup();
   115→              return;
   116→            }
   117→
   118→            settled = true;
   119→            cleanup();
   120→            resolve(blob);
   121→          },
   122→          options.type ?? 'image/jpeg',
   123→          options.quality ?? 0.85,
   124→        );
   125→      } catch (error) {
   126→        fail(error);
   127→      }
   128→    };
   129→
   130→    const handleError = () => {
   131→      fail(options.onVideoError?.(video) ?? new Error('Failed to load video'));
   132→    };
   133→
   134→    video.addEventListener('loadedmetadata', handleLoadedMetadata);
   135→    video.addEventListener('seeked', handleSeeked);
   136→    video.addEventListener('error', handleError);
   137→    video.src = url;
   138→  });
   139→}
   140→
```

    63→    structureVideo: {} as never,
    64→  };
    65→}
    66→
    67→function createHandlerState(overrides: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
    68→  return {
    69→    core: {
    70→      projectId: 'project-1',
    71→      selectedShot: { id: 'shot-1' },
    72→      simpleFilteredImages: [{ id: 'img-1' }, { id: 'img-2' }],
    73→    },
    74→    contexts: createContexts(),
    75→    mutations: {
    76→      addImageToShotMutation: { mutateAsync: vi.fn() },
    77→      removeImageFromShotMutation: { mutateAsync: vi.fn() },
    78→      loadPositions: vi.fn(),
    79→    },
    80→    ...overrides,
    81→  } as never;
    82→}
    83→
    84→describe('useApplySettingsHandler', () => {
    85→  const fakeQueryClient = { invalidateQueries: vi.fn() };
    86→
    87→  beforeEach(() => {
    88→    vi.clearAllMocks();
    89→    mocks.useQueryClient.mockReturnValue(fakeQueryClient);
    90→    mocks.fetchTask.mockResolvedValue({
    91→      status: 'found',
    92→      taskData: { id: 'task-1', params: {} },
    93→    });
    94→    mocks.extractSettings.mockReturnValue({
    95→      generation: {},
    96→      images: [],
    97→      prompts: {},
    98→      modes: {},
    99→      advanced: {},
   100→      textAddons: {},
   101→      motion: {},
   102→      loras: {},
   103→      structure: {},
   104→    });
   105→    mocks.replaceImagesIfRequested.mockResolvedValue(undefined);
   106→    mocks.applyModelSettings.mockResolvedValue(undefined);
   107→    mocks.applyPromptSettings.mockResolvedValue(undefined);
   108→    mocks.applyGenerationSettings.mockResolvedValue(undefined);
   109→    mocks.applyModeSettings.mockResolvedValue(undefined);
   110→    mocks.applyAdvancedModeSettings.mockResolvedValue(undefined);
   111→    mocks.applyTextPromptAddons.mockResolvedValue(undefined);
   112→    mocks.applyMotionSettings.mockResolvedValue(undefined);
   113→    mocks.applyLoRAs.mockResolvedValue(undefined);
   114→    mocks.applyStructureVideo.mockResolvedValue(undefined);
   115→  });
   116→
   117→  it('returns a stable callback across re-renders', () => {
   118→    const handlerState = createHandlerState();
   119→    const { result, rerender } = renderHook(() => useApplySettingsHandler(handlerState));
   120→
   121→    const firstCallback = result.current;
   122→    rerender();
   123→    expect(result.current).toBe(firstCallback);
   124→  });
   125→
   126→  it('calls applySettingsFromTask with the latest handler state via ref', async () => {
   127→    const initialState = createHandlerState();
   128→    const updatedContexts = createContexts();
   129→    updatedContexts.model = { updated: true } as never;
   130→    const updatedState = createHandlerState({ contexts: updatedContexts });
   131→
   132→    const { result, rerender } = renderHook(
   133→      ({ state }) => useApplySettingsHandler(state),
   134→      { initialProps: { state: initialState } },
   135→    );
   136→
   137→    // Update the state (simulates a re-render with new props)
   138→    rerender({ state: updatedState });
   139→
   140→    await act(async () => {
   141→      await result.current('task-42', false, ['img-a']);
   142→    });
   143→
   144→    // Should have fetched the task
   145→    expect(mocks.fetchTask).toHaveBeenCalledWith('task-42');
   146→
   147→    // Should apply model settings with the UPDATED contexts (from the ref)
   148→    expect(mocks.applyModelSettings).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
   149→      expect.anything(),
   150→      updatedContexts.model,
   151→    );
   152→  });
   153→
   154→  it('shows an error toast when simpleFilteredImages has missing IDs and replaceImages is true', async () => {
   155→    const handlerState = createHandlerState({
   156→      core: {
   157→        projectId: 'project-1',
   158→        selectedShot: { id: 'shot-1' },
   159→        simpleFilteredImages: [{ id: 'img-1' }, { id: undefined }],
   160→      },
   161→    });
   162→
   163→    const { result } = renderHook(() => useApplySettingsHandler(handlerState));
   164→
   165→    await act(async () => {
   166→      await result.current('task-99', true, ['img-x']);
   167→    });
   168→
   169→    // Should show toast error (showToast: true) and NOT proceed with applySettingsFromTask
   170→    expect(mocks.normalizeAndPresentError).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
   171→      expect.any(Error),
   172→      expect.objectContaining({
   173→        showToast: true,
   174→        toastTitle: 'Loading shot data',
   175→      }),
   176→    );
   177→    expect(mocks.fetchTask).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
   178→  });
   179→
   180→  it('does not show error toast when replaceImages is false even with missing IDs', async () => {
   181→    const handlerState = createHandlerState({
   182→      core: {
   183→        projectId: 'project-1',
   184→        selectedShot: { id: 'shot-1' },
   185→        simpleFilteredImages: [{ id: undefined }],
   186→      },
   187→    });
   188→
   189→    const { result } = renderHook(() => useApplySettingsHandler(handlerState));
   190→
   191→    await act(async () => {
   192→      await result.current('task-99', false, []);
   193→    });
   194→
   195→    // Should NOT show the loading toast - missing IDs only matter when replaceImages is true
   196→    expect(mocks.normalizeAndPresentError).not.toHaveBeenCalledWith(
   197→      expect.any(Error),
   198→      expect.objectContaining({ toastTitle: 'Loading shot data' }),
   199→    );
   200→    // Should proceed to fetch the task
   201→    expect(mocks.fetchTask).toHaveBeenCalledWith('task-99');
   202→  });
   203→
   204→  it('catches errors from applySettingsFromTask and presents them', async () => {
   205→    const taskError = new Error('Network failure');
   206→    mocks.fetchTask.mockRejectedValue(taskError);
   207→
   208→    const handlerState = createHandlerState();
   209→    const { result } = renderHook(() => useApplySettingsHandler(handlerState));
   210→
   211→    await act(async () => {
   212→      await result.current('task-fail', false, []);
   213→    });
   214→
   215→    expect(mocks.normalizeAndPresentError).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
   216→      taskError,
   217→      expect.objectContaining({
   218→        context: 'useApplySettingsHandler',
   219→        toastTitle: 'Failed to apply settings from task',
   220→      }),
   221→    );
   222→  });
   223→});

1→/**
     2→ * Stable Apply Settings Handler Hook
     3→ *
     4→ * Provides a stable callback for applying settings from tasks without
     5→ * recreating on every render.
     6→ */
     7→
     8→import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
     9→import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
    10→import * as ApplySettingsService from '../../services/applySettingsService';
    11→import type { GenerationRow, Shot } from '@/domains/generation/types';
    12→import type { LoraModel } from '@/domains/lora/types/lora';
    13→import { enqueueGenerationsInvalidation } from '@/shared/hooks/invalidation/useGenerationInvalidation';
    14→import type { AddImageToShotVariables } from '@/shared/hooks/shots/addImageToShotHelpers';
    15→import { normalizeAndPresentError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/runtimeError';
    16→
    17→interface ApplySettingsHandlerCore {
    18→  projectId: string;
    19→  selectedShot: Shot | undefined;
    20→  simpleFilteredImages: GenerationRow[];
    21→}
    22→
    23→export interface ApplySettingsHandlerContexts {
    24→  model: ApplySettingsService.ApplyModelContext;
    25→  prompts: ApplySettingsService.ApplyPromptContext;
    26→  generation: ApplySettingsService.ApplyGenerationContext;
    27→  modes: ApplySettingsService.ApplyModeContext;
    28→  advanced: ApplySettingsService.ApplyAdvancedContext;
    29→  textAddons: ApplySettingsService.ApplyTextAddonContext;
    30→  motion: ApplySettingsService.ApplyMotionContext;
    31→  loras: ApplySettingsService.ApplyLoraContext;
    32→  structureVideo: ApplySettingsService.ApplyStructureVideoContext;
    33→}
    34→
    35→interface ApplySettingsHandlerMutations {
    36→  addImageToShotMutation: {
    37→    mutateAsync: (params: AddImageToShotVariables) => Promise<unknown>;
    38→  };
    39→  removeImageFromShotMutation: {
    40→    mutateAsync: (params: {
    41→      shotId: string;
    42→      shotGenerationId: string;
    43→      projectId: string;
    44→      shiftItems?: { id: string; newFrame: number }[];
    45→    }) => Promise<unknown>;
    46→  };
    47→  loadPositions: (opts?: { silent?: boolean; reason?: string }) => void | Promise<void>;
    48→}
    49→
    50→interface ApplySettingsHandlerInput {
    51→  core: ApplySettingsHandlerCore;
    52→  contexts: ApplySettingsHandlerContexts;
    53→  mutations: ApplySettingsHandlerMutations;
    54→}
    55→
    56→async function fetchTaskData(taskId: string) {
    57→  return ApplySettingsService.fetchTask(taskId);
    58→}
    59→
    60→async function applySettingsFromTask(
    61→  taskId: string,
    62→  replaceImages: boolean,
    63→  inputImages: string[],
    64→  handlerState: ApplySettingsHandlerInput,
    65→  queryClient: ReturnType<typeof useQueryClient>,
    66→) {
    67→  const taskLookup = await fetchTaskData(taskId);
    68→  if (taskLookup.status === 'missing') {
    69→    normalizeAndPresentError(new Error('Task not found'), {
    70→      context: 'useApplySettingsHandler',
    71→      toastTitle: 'Failed to apply settings from task',
    72→      showToast: false,
    73→      logData: { taskId },
    74→    });
    75→    return;
    76→  }
    77→
    78→  const settings = ApplySettingsService.extractSettings(taskLookup.taskData);
    79→
    80→  await ApplySettingsService.replaceImagesIfRequested(
    81→    settings.generation,
    82→    settings.images,
    83→    replaceImages,
    84→    inputImages,
    85→    handlerState.core.selectedShot ?? null,
    86→    handlerState.core.projectId,
    87→    handlerState.core.simpleFilteredImages,
    88→    handlerState.mutations.addImageToShotMutation,
    89→    handlerState.mutations.removeImageFromShotMutation,
    90→  );
    91→
    92→  await ApplySettingsService.applyModelSettings(settings.generation, handlerState.contexts.model);
    93→  await ApplySettingsService.applyPromptSettings(settings.prompts, handlerState.contexts.prompts);
    94→  await ApplySettingsService.applyGenerationSettings(settings.generation, handlerState.contexts.generation);
    95→  await ApplySettingsService.applyModeSettings(settings.modes, handlerState.contexts.modes);
    96→  await ApplySettingsService.applyAdvancedModeSettings(settings.advanced, handlerState.contexts.advanced);
    97→  await ApplySettingsService.applyTextPromptAddons(settings.textAddons, handlerState.contexts.textAddons);
    98→  await ApplySettingsService.applyMotionSettings(
    99→    { ...settings.motion, advancedMode: settings.modes.advancedMode },
   100→    handlerState.contexts.motion,
   101→  );
   102→  await ApplySettingsService.applyLoRAs(
   103→    { ...settings.loras, advancedMode: settings.modes.advancedMode },
   104→    handlerState.contexts.loras,
   105→  );
   106→  await ApplySettingsService.applyStructureVideo(settings.structure, handlerState.contexts.structureVideo);
   107→
   108→  if (handlerState.core.selectedShot?.id) {
   109→    enqueueGenerationsInvalidation(queryClient, handlerState.core.selectedShot.id, {
   110→      reason: 'apply-settings-force-reload',
   111→      scope: 'all',
   112→      delayMs: 200,
   113→    });
   114→  }
   115→
   116→  await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 200));
   117→  await Promise.resolve(handlerState.mutations.loadPositions({ silent: true }));
   118→}
   119→
   120→export function useApplySettingsHandler(handlerState: ApplySettingsHandlerInput) {
   121→  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
   122→  const handlerStateRef = useRef(handlerState);
   123→
   124→  useEffect(() => {
   125→    handlerStateRef.current = handlerState;
   126→  }, [handlerState]);
   127→
   128→  return useCallback(async (taskId: string, replaceImages: boolean, inputImages: string[]) => {
   129→    const latestHandlerState = handlerStateRef.current;
   130→    const hasMissingIds = latestHandlerState.core.simpleFilteredImages.some((img) => !img.id);
   131→    if (hasMissingIds && replaceImages) {
   132→      normalizeAndPresentError(new Error('Loading shot data... please try again in a moment.'), {
   133→        context: 'useApplySettingsHandler',
   134→        toastTitle: 'Loading shot data',
   135→        showToast: true,
   136→      });
   137→      return;
   138→    }
   139→
   140→    try {
   141→      await applySettingsFromTask(taskId, replaceImages, inputImages, latestHandlerState, queryClient);
   142→    } catch (error) {
   143→      normalizeAndPresentError(error, {
   144→        context: 'useApplySettingsHandler',
   145→        toastTitle: 'Failed to apply settings from task',
   146→      });
   147→    }
   148→  }, [queryClient]);
   149→}
   150→

1→import { getSupabaseClient } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
     2→import {
     3→  enqueueSettingsWrite,
     4→  initializeSettingsWriteQueue,
     5→  type QueuedWrite,
     6→} from '@/shared/lib/settingsWriteQueue';
     7→import { deepMerge } from '@/shared/lib/utils/deepEqual';
     8→import { isCancellationError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/errorUtils';
     9→import { ToolSettingsError } from '@/shared/lib/toolSettingsService';
    10→
    11→type SettingsScopeIdentifier = 'user' | 'project' | 'shot';
    12→type SettingsScopeTableName = 'users' | 'projects' | 'shots';
    13→
    14→const SETTINGS_SCOPE_TABLES: Record<SettingsScopeIdentifier, SettingsScopeTableName> = {
    15→  user: 'users',
    16→  project: 'projects',
    17→  shot: 'shots',
    18→};
    19→
    20→function assertNeverScope(scope: never): never {
    21→  throw new Error(`Unsupported settings scope: ${String(scope)}`);
    22→}
    23→
    24→function resolveSettingsScopeTable(scope: SettingsScopeIdentifier): SettingsScopeTableName {
    25→  switch (scope) {
    26→    case 'user':
    27→    case 'project':
    28→    case 'shot':
    29→      return SETTINGS_SCOPE_TABLES[scope];
    30→    default:
    31→      return assertNeverScope(scope);
    32→  }
    33→}
    34→
    35→function selectSettingsForScope(scope: SettingsScopeIdentifier, id: string) {
    36→  const tableName = resolveSettingsScopeTable(scope);
    37→  return getSupabaseClient()
    38→    .from(tableName)
    39→    .select('settings')
    40→    .eq('id', id)
    41→    .single();
    42→}
    43→
    44→function callUpdateToolSettingsAtomicRpc(
    45→  tableName: SettingsScopeTableName,
    46→  id: string,
    47→  toolId: string,
    48→  settings: Record<string, unknown>,
    49→) {
    50→  return getSupabaseClient().rpc('update_tool_settings_atomic', {
    51→    p_table_name: tableName,
    52→    p_id: id,
    53→    p_tool_id: toolId,
    54→    p_settings: settings,
    55→  });
    56→}
    57→
    58→export type SettingsScope = 'user' | 'project' | 'shot';
    59→type SettingsWriteMode = 'debounced' | 'immediate';
    60→
    61→interface UpdateToolSettingsParams {
    62→  scope: SettingsScope;
    63→  id: string;
    64→  toolId: string;
    65→  patch: unknown;
    66→}
    67→
    68→interface AbortSignalCapable<T> {
    69→  abortSignal?: (signal: AbortSignal) => T;
    70→}
    71→
    72→function isAbortSignal(value: unknown): value is AbortSignal {
    73→  return !!value
    74→    && typeof value === 'object'
    75→    && 'aborted' in value
    76→    && typeof (value as AbortSignal).addEventListener === 'function';
    77→}
    78→
    79→function isSettingsWriteMode(value: unknown): value is SettingsWriteMode {
    80→  return value === 'debounced' || value === 'immediate';
    81→}
    82→
    83→function maybeAttachAbortSignal<T>(query: T, signal?: AbortSignal): T {
    84→  if (!signal) return query;
    85→
    86→  const candidate = query as T & AbortSignalCapable<T>;
    87→  if (typeof candidate.abortSignal === 'function') {
    88→    return candidate.abortSignal(signal);
    89→  }
    90→
    91→  return query;
    92→}
    93→
    94→function throwIfAborted(signal?: AbortSignal): void {
    95→  if (signal?.aborted) {
    96→    throw new ToolSettingsError('cancelled', 'Request was cancelled', {
    97→      recoverable: true,
    98→      cause: signal.reason,
    99→    });
   100→  }
   101→}
   102→
   103→async function fetchSettingsForScope(
   104→  scope: SettingsScope,
   105→  id: string,
   106→  signal?: AbortSignal,
   107→) {
   108→  throwIfAborted(signal);
   109→
   110→  return maybeAttachAbortSignal(selectSettingsForScope(scope, id), signal);
   111→}
   112→
   113→async function rawUpdateToolSettings(write: QueuedWrite): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
   114→  const { scope, entityId: id, toolId, patch, signal } = write;
   115→
   116→  try {
   117→    if (scope !== 'user' && scope !== 'project' && scope !== 'shot') {
   118→      throw new ToolSettingsError(
   119→        'invalid_scope_identifier',
   120→        `Invalid scope: ${scope}`,
   121→      );
   122→    }
   123→
   124→    const tableName = resolveSettingsScopeTable(scope);
   125→    const { data: currentEntity, error: fetchError } = await fetchSettingsForScope(scope, id, signal);
   126→
   127→    if (fetchError) {
   128→      const errorMessage = fetchError.message || '';
   129→      if (
   130→        errorMessage.includes('ERR_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES')
   131→        || errorMessage.includes('Failed to fetch')
   132→        || fetchError.code === 'ERR_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES'
   133→      ) {
   134→        throw new ToolSettingsError(
   135→          'network',
   136→          `Network exhaustion: ${errorMessage}`,
   137→          { recoverable: true, cause: fetchError },
   138→        );
   139→      }
   140→      throw new ToolSettingsError(
   141→        'scope_fetch_failed',
   142→        `Failed to fetch current ${scope} settings: ${errorMessage}`,
   143→        { recoverable: true, cause: fetchError },
   144→      );
   145→    }
   146→
   147→    const currentSettings = (currentEntity?.settings as Record<string, unknown>) ?? {};
   148→    const currentToolSettings = (currentSettings[toolId] as Record<string, unknown>) ?? {};
   149→    const updatedToolSettings = deepMerge({}, currentToolSettings, patch);
   150→
   151→    throwIfAborted(signal);
   152→    const { error: rpcError } = await maybeAttachAbortSignal(
   153→      callUpdateToolSettingsAtomicRpc(
   154→        tableName,
   155→        id,
   156→        toolId,
   157→        updatedToolSettings,
   158→      ),
   159→      signal,
   160→    );
   161→
   162→    if (rpcError) {
   163→      throw new ToolSettingsError(
   164→        'scope_fetch_failed',
   165→        `Failed to update ${scope} settings: ${rpcError.message}`,
   166→        { recoverable: true, cause: rpcError },
   167→      );
   168→    }
   169→
   170→    return updatedToolSettings;
   171→  } catch (error: unknown) {
   172→    if (isCancellationError(error)) {
   173→      throw new ToolSettingsError('cancelled', 'Request was cancelled', {
   174→        recoverable: true,
   175→        cause: error,
   176→      });
   177→    }
   178→
   179→    throw error;
   180→  }
   181→}
   182→
   183→export function initializeToolSettingsWriteRuntime(): void {
   184→  initializeSettingsWriteQueue(rawUpdateToolSettings);
   185→}
   186→
   187→export function updateToolSettingsSupabase(
   188→  params: UpdateToolSettingsParams,
   189→  mode?: SettingsWriteMode,
   190→): Promise<Record<string, unknown>>;
   191→export function updateToolSettingsSupabase(
   192→  params: UpdateToolSettingsParams,
   193→  signal?: AbortSignal,
   194→  mode?: SettingsWriteMode,
   195→): Promise<Record<string, unknown>>;
   196→export function updateToolSettingsSupabase(
   197→  params: UpdateToolSettingsParams,
   198→  signalOrMode?: AbortSignal | SettingsWriteMode,
   199→  maybeMode: SettingsWriteMode = 'debounced',
   200→): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
   201→  const { scope, id, toolId, patch } = params;
   202→  const signal = isAbortSignal(signalOrMode) ? signalOrMode : undefined;
   203→  const mode = isSettingsWriteMode(signalOrMode) ? signalOrMode : maybeMode;
   204→
   205→  return enqueueSettingsWrite({
   206→    scope,
   207→    entityId: id,
   208→    toolId,
   209→    patch: patch as Record<string, unknown>,
   210→    ...(signal ? { signal } : {}),
   211→  }, mode, rawUpdateToolSettings) as Promise<Record<string, unknown>>;
   212→}
   213→

    35→import { GRID_COLUMN_CLASSES, calculateGalleryLayout } from '@/shared/components/MediaGallery/utils';

    37→interface UseAspectRatioLayoutParams {
    38→  projectAspectRatio?: string;
    39→  isMobile: boolean;
    40→  containerWidth: number;
    41→  reducedSpacing: boolean;
    42→  columnsPerRow: 'auto' | number;
    43→  itemsPerPage?: number;
    44→}
    45→
    46→function useAspectRatioLayout({
    47→  projectAspectRatio,
    48→  isMobile,
    49→  containerWidth,
    50→  reducedSpacing,
    51→  columnsPerRow,
    52→  itemsPerPage,
    53→}: UseAspectRatioLayoutParams) {
    54→  const aspectRatioLayout = useMemo(
    55→    () => calculateGalleryLayout(projectAspectRatio, isMobile, containerWidth, undefined, reducedSpacing),
    56→    [projectAspectRatio, isMobile, containerWidth, reducedSpacing],
    57→  );
    58→
    59→  const effectiveColumnsPerRow = columnsPerRow === 'auto' ? aspectRatioLayout.columns : columnsPerRow;
    60→  const defaultItemsPerPage = aspectRatioLayout.itemsPerPage;
    61→
    62→  const rawItemsPerPage = itemsPerPage ?? defaultItemsPerPage;
    63→  const actualItemsPerPage =
    64→    Math.floor(rawItemsPerPage / effectiveColumnsPerRow) * effectiveColumnsPerRow || effectiveColumnsPerRow;
    65→
    66→  const gridColumnClasses = useMemo(
    67→    () =>
    68→      GRID_COLUMN_CLASSES[effectiveColumnsPerRow as keyof typeof GRID_COLUMN_CLASSES] ||
    69→      aspectRatioLayout.gridColumnClasses,
    70→    [effectiveColumnsPerRow, aspectRatioLayout.gridColumnClasses],
    71→  );
    72→
    73→  return {
    74→    aspectRatioLayout,
    75→    effectiveColumnsPerRow,
    76→    actualItemsPerPage,
    77→    gridColumnClasses,
    78→  };
    79→}
    80→

   466→      </div>

   481→        />

    21→import { parseRatio } from '@/shared/lib/media/aspectRatios';
    22→
    23→const MIN_PADDING = 60;
    24→const MAX_PADDING = 200;
    25→
    26→function clampPadding(value: number): number {
    27→  return Math.min(Math.max(value, MIN_PADDING), MAX_PADDING);
    28→}
    29→
    30→function extractResolutionFromMetadata(image: GeneratedImageWithMetadata): { width?: number; height?: number } {
    31→  let width = image.metadata?.width;
    32→  let height = image.metadata?.height;
    33→
    34→  if (width && height) {
    35→    return { width, height };
    36→  }
    37→
    38→  const resolution = image.metadata?.originalParams?.orchestrator_details?.resolution;
    39→  if (resolution && typeof resolution === 'string' && resolution.includes('x')) {
    40→    const [parsedWidth, parsedHeight] = resolution.split('x').map(Number);
    41→    if (!isNaN(parsedWidth) && !isNaN(parsedHeight)) {
    42→      width = parsedWidth;
    43→      height = parsedHeight;
    44→    }
    45→  }
    46→
    47→  return { width, height };
    48→}
    49→
    50→function resolveAspectRatioPadding(
    51→  image: GeneratedImageWithMetadata,
    52→  projectAspectRatio?: string,
    53→): string {
    54→  if (projectAspectRatio) {
    55→    const ratio = parseRatio(projectAspectRatio);
    56→    if (!isNaN(ratio)) {
    57→      const calculatedPadding = (1 / ratio) * 100;
    58→      return `${clampPadding(calculatedPadding)}%`;
    59→    }
    60→  }
    61→
    62→  const { width, height } = extractResolutionFromMetadata(image);
    63→  if (width && height) {
    64→    return `${clampPadding((height / width) * 100)}%`;
    65→  }
    66→
    67→  return '100%';
    68→}

    80→import { deriveGalleryInputImages } from "./MediaGallery/utils";
    81→import { isImageEditTaskType } from "@/shared/lib/taskParamsUtils";
    82→import { useMarkVariantViewed } from "@/shared/hooks/variants/useMarkVariantViewed";
    83→import { getGenerationId } from '@/shared/lib/media/mediaTypeHelpers';
    84→export const MediaGalleryItem: React.FC<MediaGalleryItemProps> = ({
    85→  image,
    86→  index,
    87→  shotWorkflow,
    88→  mobileInteraction,
    89→  features,
    90→  actions,
    91→  loading,
    92→  projectAspectRatio,
    93→  dataTour,
    94→}) => {
    95→  const {
    96→    selectedShotIdLocal,
    97→    simplifiedShotOptions,
    98→    setSelectedShotIdLocal,
    99→    setLastAffectedShotId,
   100→    showTickForImageId,
   101→    onShowTick,
   102→    onShowSecondaryTick,
   103→    optimisticUnpositionedIds,
   104→    optimisticPositionedIds,
   105→    onOptimisticUnpositioned,
   106→    onOptimisticPositioned,
   107→    addingToShotImageId,
   108→    setAddingToShotImageId,
   109→    addingToShotWithoutPositionImageId,
   110→    setAddingToShotWithoutPositionImageId,
   111→    currentViewingShotId,
   112→    onCreateShot,
   113→    onAddToLastShot,
   114→    onAddToLastShotWithoutPosition,
   115→  } = shotWorkflow;
   116→  const {
   117→    isMobile,
   118→    mobileActiveImageId,
   119→    mobilePopoverOpenImageId,
   120→    onMobileTap,
   121→  } = mobileInteraction;
   122→  const {
   123→    showShare = true,
   124→    showDelete = true,
   125→    showEdit = true,
   126→    showStar = true,
   127→    showAddToShot = true,
   128→    enableSingleClick = false,
   129→    videosAsThumbnails = false,
   130→  } = features;
   131→  const {
   132→    onOpenLightbox,
   133→    onDelete,
   134→    onToggleStar,
   135→    onImageClick,
   136→    onImageLoaded,
   137→  } = actions;
   138→  const {
   139→    shouldLoad = true,
   140→    isPriority = false,
   141→    isDeleting,
   142→  } = loading;
   143→  const {
   144→    localStarred,
   145→    setLocalStarred,
   146→    isTogglingStar,
   147→    setIsTogglingStar,
   148→    isInfoOpen,
   149→    setIsInfoOpen,
   150→    isShotSelectorOpen,
   151→    setIsShotSelectorOpen,
   152→    isDragging,
   153→    setIsDragging,
   154→    isCreateShotModalOpen,
   155→    setIsCreateShotModalOpen,
   156→    isCreatingShot,
   157→    handleCreateShot,
   158→  } = useMediaGalleryItemState({ image, onCreateShot });
   159→  const prefetchTaskData = usePrefetchTaskData();
   160→  const taskIdFromMetadata = image.metadata?.taskId as string | undefined;
   161→  const actualGenerationId = getGenerationId(image);
   162→  const generationIdForActions = actualGenerationId || image.id;
   163→  const { selectedProjectId } = useProjectSelectionContext();
   164→  const { data: taskIdMapping } = useGenerationTaskMapping(actualGenerationId ?? '');
   165→  const taskIdFromCache = typeof taskIdMapping?.taskId === 'string' ? taskIdMapping.taskId : null;
   166→  const taskId: string | null = taskIdFromMetadata || taskIdFromCache;
   167→  const { data: taskData } = useGetTask(taskId ?? '', selectedProjectId ?? null);
   168→  const handleMouseEnter = useCallback(() => {
   169→    if (!isMobile && actualGenerationId) {
   170→      prefetchTaskData(actualGenerationId);
   171→    }
   172→  }, [isMobile, actualGenerationId, prefetchTaskData]);
   173→  const inputImages = useMemo(() => deriveGalleryInputImages(taskData), [taskData]);
   174→  const taskType = taskData?.taskType;
   175→  const { data: taskTypeInfo } = useTaskType(taskType || '');
   176→  const isVideoTask = taskTypeInfo?.content_type === 'video' ||
   177→    (!taskTypeInfo && image.metadata?.tool_type === TOOL_IDS.TRAVEL_BETWEEN_IMAGES);
   178→  const isImageEditTask = isImageEditTaskType(taskType || undefined);
   179→  const shouldShowTaskDetails = (!!taskData) && (isVideoTask || isImageEditTask);
   180→  const { handleShare, isCreatingShare, shareCopied, shareSlug } = useShareGeneration(image.id, taskId);
   181→  const { markAllViewed } = useMarkVariantViewed();
   182→  const handleMarkAllVariantsViewed = useCallback(() => {
   183→    if (actualGenerationId) {
   184→      markAllViewed(actualGenerationId);
   185→    }
   186→  }, [actualGenerationId, markAllViewed]);
   187→  const { navigateToShot } = useShotNavigation();
   188→  const { setLastAffectedShotId: updateLastAffectedShotId } = useLastAffectedShot();
   189→  const {
   190→    quickCreateSuccess,
   191→    handleQuickCreateAndAdd,
   192→    handleVisitCreatedShot,
   193→  } = useQuickShotCreate({
   194→    generationId: generationIdForActions,
   195→    generationPreview: {
   196→      imageUrl: image.url,
   197→      thumbUrl: image.thumbUrl,
   198→      type: image.type,
   199→      location: image.location,
   200→    },
   201→    shots: simplifiedShotOptions,
   202→    onShotChange: (shotId) => {
   203→      updateLastAffectedShotId(shotId);
   204→      setSelectedShotIdLocal(shotId);
   205→    },
   206→    onLoadingStart: () => setAddingToShotImageId(image.id),
   207→    onLoadingEnd: () => setAddingToShotImageId(null),
   208→  });
   209→  const {
   210→    isVideoContent,
   211→    displayUrl,
   212→    stableDisplayUrl,
   213→    stableVideoUrl,
   214→    progressiveEnabled,
   215→    isThumbShowing,
   216→    isFullLoaded,
   217→    progressiveRef,
   218→  } = useStableMediaUrls({ image, isPriority });
   219→  const {
   220→    actualSrc,
   221→    actualDisplayUrl,
   222→    imageLoaded,
   223→    imageLoadError,
   224→    handleImageLoad,
   225→    handleImageError,
   226→    retryImageLoad,
   227→    setImageLoading,
   228→  } = useImageLoading({
   229→    image,
   230→    displayUrl,
   231→    shouldLoad,
   232→    onImageLoaded,
   233→  });
   234→  const { addToShot, addToShotWithoutPosition } = useMediaGalleryItemShotActions({
   235→    imageId: image.id,
   236→    generationId: generationIdForActions,
   237→    imageUrl: image.url,
   238→    thumbUrl: image.thumbUrl ?? image.url,
   239→    displayUrl: displayUrl || image.url,
   240→    selectedShotId: selectedShotIdLocal,
   241→    isMobile,
   242→    onAddToLastShot,
   243→    onAddToLastShotWithoutPosition,
   244→    onShowTick,
   245→    onShowSecondaryTick,
   246→    onOptimisticPositioned,
   247→    onOptimisticUnpositioned,
   248→    setAddingToShotImageId,
   249→    setAddingToShotWithoutPositionImageId,
   250→  });
   251→  const shouldShowMetadata = useMemo(() => {
   252→    if (!image.metadata) return false;
   253→    return isMobile
   254→      ? (mobilePopoverOpenImageId === image.id)
   255→      : isInfoOpen;
   256→  }, [image.metadata, isMobile, mobilePopoverOpenImageId, image.id, isInfoOpen]);
   257→  const isCurrentDeleting = isDeleting === true || isDeleting === image.id;
   258→  const imageKey = image.id || `image-${actualDisplayUrl}-${index}`;
   259→  const isPlaceholder = !image.id && actualDisplayUrl === "/placeholder.svg";
   260→  const currentTargetShotName = selectedShotIdLocal ? simplifiedShotOptions.find(s => s.id === selectedShotIdLocal)?.name : undefined;
   261→  const handleDragStart = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {
   262→    if (isMobile) {
   263→      e.preventDefault();
   264→      return;
   265→    }
   266→    setIsDragging(true);
   267→    setGenerationDragData(e, {
   268→      generationId: image.id,
   269→      imageUrl: image.url,
   270→      thumbUrl: image.thumbUrl,
   271→      metadata: image.metadata
   272→    });
   273→    const cleanup = createDragPreview(e);
   274→    if (cleanup) {
   275→      setTimeout(cleanup, 0);
   276→    }
   277→  }, [image, isMobile, setIsDragging]);
   278→  const handleDragEnd = useCallback(() => {
   279→    setIsDragging(false);
   280→  }, [setIsDragging]);
   281→  const { handleTouchStart, handleInteraction } = useItemInteraction({
   282→    image,
   283→    isMobile,
   284→    mobileActiveImageId,
   285→    enableSingleClick,
   286→    onImageClick,
   287→    onMobileTap,
   288→  });
   289→  const {
   290→    isAlreadyPositionedInSelectedShot,
   291→    isAlreadyAssociatedWithoutPosition,
   292→    shouldShowAddWithoutPositionButton,
   293→  } = useShotPositionChecks({
   294→    image,
   295→    selectedShotIdLocal,
   296→    currentViewingShotId,
   297→    optimisticPositionedIds,
   298→    optimisticUnpositionedIds,
   299→    onAddToLastShotWithoutPosition,
   300→    showTickForImageId,
   301→    addingToShotImageId,
   302→  });
   303→  const aspectRatioPadding = resolveAspectRatioPadding(image, projectAspectRatio);
   304→  const minHeight = '120px'; // Minimum height for very small images
   305→  if (isPlaceholder) {
   306→    return (
   307→      <div
   308→        key={imageKey}
   309→        className="border rounded-lg overflow-hidden bg-muted animate-pulse"
   310→      >
   311→        <div style={{ paddingBottom: aspectRatioPadding }} className="relative">
   312→          <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
   313→            <Eye className="h-12 w-12 text-muted-foreground opacity-30" />
   314→          </div>
   315→        </div>
   316→      </div>
   317→    );
   318→  }
   319→  const imageContent = (
   320→    <div
   321→        className={`border rounded-lg overflow-hidden hover:shadow-md transition-all duration-200 relative group bg-card ${
   322→          isDragging ? 'opacity-50 scale-75' : ''
   323→        } ${!isMobile ? 'cursor-grab active:cursor-grabbing' : ''}`}
   324→        draggable={!isMobile}
   325→        onDragStart={handleDragStart}
   326→        onDragEnd={handleDragEnd}
   327→        onMouseEnter={handleMouseEnter}
   328→        data-tour={dataTour}
   329→        onTouchStart={isMobile && !enableSingleClick && !isVideoContent ? handleTouchStart : undefined}
   330→        onTouchEnd={isMobile && !enableSingleClick && !isVideoContent ? handleInteraction : undefined}
   331→    >
   332→      {/* Image layer */}
   333→      <div
   334→        style={{
   335→          paddingBottom: aspectRatioPadding,
   336→          minHeight: minHeight
   337→        }}
   338→        className="relative bg-muted/50"
   339→      >
   340→          {isVideoContent ? (
   341→            <VideoContent
   342→              image={image}
   343→              stableDisplayUrl={stableDisplayUrl}
   344→              stableVideoUrl={stableVideoUrl}
   345→              actualSrc={actualSrc}
   346→              shouldLoad={shouldLoad}
   347→              imageLoaded={imageLoaded}
   348→              videosAsThumbnails={videosAsThumbnails}
   349→              isMobile={isMobile}
   350→              enableSingleClick={enableSingleClick}
   351→              onImageClick={onImageClick}
   352→              onOpenLightbox={onOpenLightbox}
   353→              onTouchStart={handleTouchStart}
   354→              onTouchEnd={handleInteraction}
   355→              onVideoError={handleImageError}
   356→              onLoadStart={() => setImageLoading(true)}
   357→              onLoadedData={handleImageLoad}
   358→            />
   359→          ) : (
   360→            <ImageContent
   361→              image={image}
   362→              index={index}
   363→              actualSrc={actualSrc}
   364→              imageLoaded={imageLoaded}
   365→              imageLoadError={imageLoadError}
   366→              progressiveEnabled={progressiveEnabled}
   367→              isThumbShowing={isThumbShowing}
   368→              isFullLoaded={isFullLoaded}
   369→              progressiveRef={progressiveRef}
   370→              isMobile={isMobile}
   371→              enableSingleClick={enableSingleClick}
   372→              onImageClick={onImageClick}
   373→              onOpenLightbox={onOpenLightbox}
   374→              onImageLoad={handleImageLoad}
   375→              onImageError={handleImageError}
   376→              onRetry={retryImageLoad}
   377→              setImageLoading={setImageLoading}
   378→            />
   379→          )}
   380→      </div>
   381→      {/* Overlay layer — single container above the image for all UI controls */}
   382→      {image.id && (
   383→      <div className="absolute inset-0 z-10 pointer-events-none [&>*]:pointer-events-auto">
   384→          <ItemShotBadges
   385→            image={image}
   386→            isVideoContent={isVideoContent}
   387→            simplifiedShotOptions={simplifiedShotOptions}
   388→            onMarkAllVariantsViewed={handleMarkAllVariantsViewed}
   389→            onNavigateToShot={(shotId) => {
   390→              const targetShot = simplifiedShotOptions.find((shot) => shot.id === shotId);
   391→              if (targetShot) {
   392→                navigateToShot(targetShot as Shot, { scrollToTop: true });
   393→              }
   394→            }}
   395→          />
   396→          {/* Add to Shot UI - Top Left (for non-video content) */}
   397→          {showAddToShot && simplifiedShotOptions.length > 0 && onAddToLastShot && (
   398→            <ShotActions
   399→              image={image}
   400→              selector={{
   401→                selectedShotId: selectedShotIdLocal,
   402→                simplifiedShotOptions,
   403→                isShotSelectorOpen,
   404→                setIsShotSelectorOpen,
   405→                setSelectedShotIdLocal,
   406→                setLastAffectedShotId,
   407→              }}
   408→              status={{
   409→                isMobile,
   410→                isVideoContent,
   411→                addingToShotImageId,
   412→                addingToShotWithoutPositionImageId: addingToShotWithoutPositionImageId ?? null,
   413→                showTickForImageId,
   414→                isAlreadyPositionedInSelectedShot,
   415→                isAlreadyAssociatedWithoutPosition,
   416→                shouldShowAddWithoutPositionButton,
   417→                currentTargetShotName,
   418→              }}
   419→              quickCreate={{
   420→                quickCreateSuccess,
   421→                handleQuickCreateAndAdd,
   422→                handleVisitCreatedShot,
   423→              }}
   424→              actions={{
   425→                onCreateShot,
   426→                onNavigateToShot: (shot) => navigateToShot(shot, { scrollToTop: true }),
   427→                onAddToShot: addToShot,
   428→                onAddToShotWithoutPosition: addToShotWithoutPosition,
   429→              }}
   430→            />
   431→          )}
   432→          {/* Timestamp - Top Right (hides on hover for images, stays visible for videos) */}
   433→          <TimeStamp
   434→            createdAt={image.createdAt}
   435→            position="top-right"
   436→            showOnHover={false}
   437→            hideOnHover={!isVideoContent}
   438→            className=""
   439→          />
   440→          <ItemMetadataBar
   441→            image={image}
   442→            isVideoContent={isVideoContent}
   443→            isMobile={isMobile}
   444→            taskData={taskData}
   445→            inputImages={inputImages}
   446→            shouldShowMetadata={shouldShowMetadata}
   447→            shouldShowTaskDetails={shouldShowTaskDetails}
   448→            setIsInfoOpen={setIsInfoOpen}
   449→            showShare={showShare}
   450→            taskId={taskId}
   451→            handleShare={handleShare}
   452→            isCreatingShare={isCreatingShare}
   453→            shareCopied={shareCopied}
   454→            shareSlug={shareSlug}
   455→            onMarkAllVariantsViewed={handleMarkAllVariantsViewed}
   456→          />
   457→          <ActionButtons
   458→            image={image}
   459→            localStarred={localStarred}
   460→            isTogglingStar={isTogglingStar}
   461→            isDeleting={isCurrentDeleting}
   462→            showStar={showStar}
   463→            showEdit={showEdit}
   464→            showDelete={showDelete}
   465→            onToggleStar={onToggleStar}
   466→            setIsTogglingStar={setIsTogglingStar}
   467→            setLocalStarred={setLocalStarred}
   468→            onOpenLightbox={onOpenLightbox}
   469→            onDelete={onDelete}
   470→          />
   471→      </div>)
   472→      }
   473→    </div>
   474→  );
   475→  return isMobile ? (
   476→    <React.Fragment key={imageKey}>
   477→      {imageContent}
   478→      {onCreateShot && (
   479→        <CreateShotModal
   480→          isOpen={isCreateShotModalOpen}
   481→          onClose={() => setIsCreateShotModalOpen(false)}
   482→          onSubmit={handleCreateShot}
   483→          isLoading={isCreatingShot}
   484→          projectId={selectedProjectId ?? undefined}
   485→        />
   486→      )}
   487→    </React.Fragment>
   488→  ) : (
   489→    <DraggableImage key={`draggable-${imageKey}`} image={image} onDoubleClick={() => onOpenLightbox(image)}>
   490→      {imageContent}
   491→      {onCreateShot && (
   492→        <CreateShotModal
   493→          isOpen={isCreateShotModalOpen}
   494→          onClose={() => setIsCreateShotModalOpen(false)}
   495→          onSubmit={handleCreateShot}
   496→          isLoading={isCreatingShot}
   497→          projectId={selectedProjectId ?? undefined}
   498→        />
   499→      )}
   500→    </DraggableImage>
   501→  );
   502→};
   503→

1→import { act, renderHook } from '@testing-library/react';
     2→import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
     3→import { useOnboardingSteps } from './useOnboardingSteps';
     4→
     5→const STEP_COUNT = 6;
     6→const EXPECTED_TITLES = ['Welcome', 'Community', 'Generation', 'Theme', 'Privacy', 'Complete'];
     7→
     8→afterEach(() => {
     9→  vi.useRealTimers();
    10→});
    11→
    12→describe('useOnboardingSteps', () => {
    13→  it('starts at step 1 with titles and first step definition', () => {
    14→    const { result } = renderHook(() => useOnboardingSteps(true));
    15→
    16→    expect(result.current.currentStep).toBe(1);
    17→    expect(result.current.currentStepDefinition.id).toBe(1);
    18→    expect(result.current.stepTitles).toEqual(EXPECTED_TITLES);
    19→  });
    20→
    21→  it('moves forward and backward within valid bounds', () => {
    22→    const { result } = renderHook(() => useOnboardingSteps(true));
    23→
    24→    act(() => {
    25→      result.current.handleBack();
    26→    });
    27→    expect(result.current.currentStep).toBe(1);
    28→
    29→    for (let i = 0; i < STEP_COUNT + 2; i += 1) {
    30→      act(() => {
    31→        result.current.handleNext();
    32→      });
    33→    }
    34→    expect(result.current.currentStep).toBe(STEP_COUNT);
    35→
    36→    act(() => {
    37→      result.current.handleBack();
    38→    });
    39→    expect(result.current.currentStep).toBe(STEP_COUNT - 1);
    40→  });
    41→
    42→  it('resets to step 1 when modal opens', () => {
    43→    const { result, rerender } = renderHook(({ isOpen }) => useOnboardingSteps(isOpen), {
    44→      initialProps: { isOpen: false },
    45→    });
    46→
    47→    act(() => {
    48→      result.current.handleNext();
    49→    });
    50→    expect(result.current.currentStep).toBe(2);
    51→
    52→    rerender({ isOpen: true });
    53→    expect(result.current.currentStep).toBe(1);
    54→  });
    55→
    56→  it('toggles shake state and clears it after timeout', () => {
    57→    vi.useFakeTimers();
    58→    const { result } = renderHook(() => useOnboardingSteps(true));
    59→
    60→    act(() => {
    61→      result.current.handleShake();
    62→    });
    63→    expect(result.current.isShaking).toBe(true);
    64→
    65→    act(() => {
    66→      vi.advanceTimersByTime(499);
    67→    });
    68→    expect(result.current.isShaking).toBe(true);
    69→
    70→    act(() => {
    71→      vi.advanceTimersByTime(1);
    72→    });
    73→    expect(result.current.isShaking).toBe(false);
    74→  });
    75→});
    76→

The module exports `buildShotEditorContextInput`, `buildShotEditorScreenModel`, `useShotEditorLayoutModel`, plus interfaces. The test expects `['buildShotEditorContextInput', 'buildShotEditorScreenModel', 'useShotEditorLayoutModel']` -- that's correct (interfaces/types aren't runtime exports).

Now let me verify the `travelContractData.ts` behavior change is semantically equivalent to the old code. The key subtle thing is the `structure_videos` fallback from `guidanceVideos`.

Looking at the old code vs new code for this specific case:

**Old code:** 
1. First reads `structure_guidance` 
2. Extracts `guidanceVideos = asRecord(structureGuidance)?.videos`
3. When checking `structure_videos`: if `pickFirstDefinedValue` returns undefined for `structure_videos`, falls back to `guidanceVideos` if it's an array

**New code:**
1. Loop sets `result.structure_guidance` and `result.structure_videos` from their respective lookups
2. After the loop, if `result.structure_videos === undefined && result.structure_guidance !== undefined`, checks `asRecord(result.structure_guidance)?.videos`

This is semantically equivalent. The order in `STRUCTURE_FIELDS` has `structure_guidance` at index 0 and `structure_videos` at index 1, so `structure_guidance` is always set first before `structure_videos` is checked. The fallback logic is preserved.

- `vi.hoisted` is used properly for all mock functions.
- `vi.mock` calls reference the hoisted mocks correctly.
- `vi.clearAllMocks()` is in `beforeEach` -- no mock leaks between tests.
- The `buildVideoLightboxLayoutState` mock is re-set in `beforeEach` with a default return value, and individual tests override it when needed. Good.

### Test descriptions vs. actual behavior: Accurate
Each description matches what the test actually checks.

### Issues found:

1. **Mostly property-mapping smoke tests.** Tests 1-4 (`pendingTaskCount`, `onMarkAllViewed`, `effectiveVideoUrl`, `hasNext/hasPrevious`) verify that a value from an input parameter lands at the correct output path. This is literally testing that `useMemo` assembles an object correctly. These are the weakest kind of tests -- they'd break on any restructure of the output shape but don't validate any logic or branching. The source hook is ~400 lines and is essentially a big "assemble objects" function with very little branching, so this is somewhat inherent to what's being tested.

2. **The panel-rendering tests (lines 277-367) are the most valuable.** They test the actual conditional branching logic in `controlsPanelContent`: VideoEditPanel vs InfoPanel vs undefined based on `showPanel`, `isInVideoEditMode`, and `videoEditSubMode`. These test real behavior.

3. **Missing coverage for interesting logic:**
   - `panelTaskId` fallback chain (line 186-188): `adjustedTaskDetailsData?.taskId || media.source_task_id || null`. Not tested.
   - The `workflowBar` construction has a significant conditional (shotWorkflow?.onAddToShot presence determines shotSelector shape). Not tested.
   - `accessibilityTitle` uses `media.id.substring(0, 8)`. Not tested (minor).
   - `onLoadVariantImages` is conditionally undefined when `editModel.variantSegmentImages` is falsy (line 65). Not tested.

4. **`as never` casts everywhere.** The factory functions cast all return values `as never`, which means TypeScript won't catch if the test fixtures drift out of sync with the actual types. If the source hook adds a required field, the test would still compile but might fail at runtime with confusing errors. This is a pragmatic tradeoff for a complex type, but worth noting.

5. **No test for `layoutProps` output.** The hook returns `layoutProps` which wires up `handleDownload`, `handleDelete`, button groups, etc. None of this is tested.

### Verdict
The panel-content tests (3 of 7) test real branching logic. The other 4 are pure property-mapping assertions that provide low value -- they essentially test that `a.b = x; return { a: { b: a.b } }` works. Not harmful, but not catching bugs either.

---

## File 2: `useApplySettingsHandler.test.ts`

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/hooks/actions/useApplySettingsHandler.test.ts`

### Mock setup: Correct
- `vi.hoisted` used properly.
- All service functions are individually mocked with pass-through wrappers.
- `vi.clearAllMocks()` in `beforeEach` -- no mock leaks.
- Default mock return values are set up in `beforeEach` so each test starts from a clean, working state.

### Test descriptions vs. actual behavior: Accurate
All descriptions match what's tested.

### Issues found:

1. **Test 2 (ref freshness, lines 126-152) is genuinely valuable.** It validates the core purpose of the hook -- that the `useRef` pattern provides a stable callback that still uses the latest state. This tests real behavior that could regress.

2. **Test 1 (stable callback, lines 117-124) is good.** Verifies the `useCallback` identity stability, which is the hook's stated purpose.

3. **Test 3 (missing IDs with replaceImages=true, lines 154-178) is solid.** Tests a real guard condition: when `simpleFilteredImages` has items with missing IDs and `replaceImages` is true, it should show an error and bail early (not call `fetchTask`). The assertion on `fetchTask.not.toHaveBeenCalled()` is particularly good -- it verifies the early return.

4. **Test 4 (missing IDs with replaceImages=false, lines 180-202) is the mirror test.** Verifies the guard only fires when `replaceImages` is true. Good.

5. **Test 5 (error handling, lines 204-222) tests the catch block.** Verifies that errors from `applySettingsFromTask` are caught and presented. Good.

6. **Missing coverage:**
   - The `taskLookup.status === 'missing'` branch (source line 68-76) is never tested. What happens when `fetchTask` returns `{ status: 'missing' }`? The hook should call `normalizeAndPresentError` with `showToast: false` and NOT proceed to apply settings. This is a real code path that could regress.
   - The `enqueueGenerationsInvalidation` call (source line 108-113) is never asserted. The mock is set up but never checked.
   - The `loadPositions` call at the end of the happy path (source line 117) is never verified.
   - The test for "ref freshness" (test 2) only checks `applyModelSettings` was called with updated contexts, but doesn't verify any of the other 8 apply functions. This is fine since they all follow the same pattern, but `replaceImagesIfRequested` has a different argument structure that could be worth one assertion.

7. **`as never` casts in factories.** Same concern as file 1 -- type safety is bypassed.

8. **Minor: `useQueryClient` mock pattern.** The mock returns a `fakeQueryClient` but no test ever asserts that `invalidateQueries` was called or that `enqueueGenerationsInvalidation` received it. The mock is set up but unused.

### Verdict
This is a meaningfully better test file. 4 of 5 tests exercise real branching logic or behavioral invariants (callback stability, ref freshness, guard conditions, error handling). The main gap is the missing `status === 'missing'` branch test and the lack of any assertions on the post-apply steps (invalidation, loadPositions).

---

## Summary

**useVideoLightboxRenderModel.test.tsx:** Mixed quality. The 3 panel-content tests are good; the 4 property-mapping tests are low-value smoke tests. Significant untested branching logic in `panelTaskId` fallback and `workflowBar` conditional. Mock setup is clean.

**useApplySettingsHandler.test.ts:** Good quality overall. Tests meaningful behavior (callback stability, ref freshness, guard conditions, error handling). Main gap: no test for the `status === 'missing'` branch, and no assertions on post-apply side effects (invalidation, loadPositions). Mock setup is clean.

Neither file has mock leaks, incorrect `vi.hoisted`/`vi.mock` patterns, or fragile assertions that test implementation details rather than behavior. The `as never` type casts are a shared weakness that trades compile-time safety for test authoring convenience.
